### [1] [Lagrangian Extensions of Newtonian Gravity constrained by Solar System tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04077)
*Pedro H. Dalprá,Júlio C. Fabris,Hermano Velten,Júnior D. Toniato*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper proposes a modified Newtonian gravity theory by introducing a second dynamical scalar field via a generalized Lagrangian, leading to distinct inertial and gravitational masses. Using weak-field approximations, it derives an effective post-Newtonian potential and constrains the model using the Nordtvedt effect and Mercury’s perihelion precession.
\n\n
本文通过引入一个广义拉格朗日量中的第二动力学标量场，提出了一种修正的牛顿引力理论，导致惯性质量与引力质量不再等价。利用弱场近似，推导出有效的后牛顿势，并通过Nordtvedt效应和水星近日点进动对模型参数进行观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from exploring alternatives to General Relativity that retain a Newtonian framework but incorporate relativistic features through additional scalar degrees of freedom, particularly inspired by theories with variable gravitational coupling. Such extensions aim to reconcile observational discrepancies or provide testable deviations from standard gravity.
\n\n
该研究的动机源于探索保留牛顿框架但通过额外标量自由度引入相对论特征的广义相对论替代理论，尤其受变引力耦合理论的启发。此类扩展旨在调和观测差异或提供可检验的标准引力偏离。

Method: The authors construct a generalized Lagrangian with two dynamical scalar fields, derive the full field equations, and apply a weak-field, slow-motion approximation to obtain an effective post-Newtonian gravitational potential. They formulate N-body equations of motion exhibiting a violation of the equivalence principle (i.e., $m_{\\text{inertial}} \\neq m_{\\text{grav}}$). Constraints are obtained via: (1) the Nordtvedt effect, which probes composition-dependent free-fall differences, and (2) secular pericenter precession computed using the method of osculating orbits for a two-body system, compared against Mercury’s observed perihelion shift.
\n\n
作者构建包含两个动力学标量场的广义拉格朗日量，推导完整的场方程，并应用弱场慢动近似以获得有效的后牛顿引力势。他们建立了体现等效原理破缺（即 $m_{\\text{inertial}} \\neq m_{\\text{grav}}$）的N体运动方程。约束来自：(1) Nordtvedt效应，探测与成分相关的自由落体差异；(2) 利用密切轨道法计算二体系统的近日点长期进动，并与水星观测到的近日点位移进行对比。

Result: The model yields a post-Newtonian potential that incorporates relativistic-like corrections and predicts a measurable Nordtvedt effect. The secular pericenter shift for Mercury is computed analytically, providing a bound on the theory's free parameter. Specific example theories within this framework are also outlined, demonstrating its flexibility.
\n\n
该模型导出了包含类相对论修正项的后牛顿势，并预言了可观测的Nordtvedt效应。水星近日点的长期进动被解析计算，从而对理论的自由参数给出了限制。文中还列举了该框架下的若干具体理论实例，展示了其灵活性。

Conclusion: The extended scalar-tensor Newtonian framework successfully embeds key post-Newtonian features while offering testable predictions through solar system experiments. Observational data from the Nordtvedt effect and Mercury’s orbit tightly constrain the deviation from standard gravity, supporting the viability of such scalar-modified theories only within narrow parameter ranges.
\n\n
该扩展的标量-张量牛顿框架成功嵌入了关键的后牛顿特征，并通过太阳系实验提供了可检验的预言。Nordtvedt效应和水星轨道的观测数据对偏离标准引力的程度施加了严格限制，表明此类标量修正理论仅在狭窄的参数范围内可行。

Abstract: We explore an extension to Newtonian gravity through a generalised Lagrangian function with the introduction of a second dynamical scalar field. Building on previous research into gravity with variable gravitational coupling, the work derives the complete field equations and applies a weak-field approximation. This leads to an effective post-Newtonian gravitational potential that includes key aspects of relativistic theories. The resulting N-body equations of motion highlight differences among inertial and gravitational masses, which can constrain the theory's free parameter through data from the Nordtvedt effect. By employing the method of osculating orbits for a two-body system, the study calculates the secular variation of the orbital pericenter and aligns this with the latest data on Mercury's perihelion shift, for another observational constraint on the model. Furthermore, a few examples of theories are discussed.

</details>


### [2] [Gravitationally Induced Quantum Decoherence of Macroscopic Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04099)
*Hiroki Takeda,Shogo Tomizuka,Takahiro Tanaka*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper develops a quantum field-theoretic framework to compute gravitationally induced decoherence of a massive particle in spatial superposition, showing that Newtonian gravity contributes a subleading logarithmic decoherence effect compared to standard collisional decoherence.
\n\n
本文构建了一个量子场论框架，用于计算处于空间叠加态的大质量粒子由引力引起的退相干效应，并表明在牛顿引力作用下，其退相干贡献是对数形式的次主导项，远小于常规碰撞退相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding whether gravity can induce quantum decoherence is crucial for foundational questions in quantum mechanics and potential tests of quantum gravity phenomenology. The authors aim to rigorously derive gravitational decoherence from first principles within a quantum field theory framework, avoiding ad hoc assumptions.
\n\n
理解引力是否能引起量子退相干对于量子力学基础问题以及量子引力现象学的潜在检验至关重要。作者旨在基于量子场论框架从第一性原理严格推导引力退相干，避免使用临时假设。

Method: The authors employ the closed-time-path (CTP) formalism to integrate out an environmental scalar field coupled to gravity, yielding an influence functional for the system particle. In the nonrelativistic and quasi-static limit, they express the decoherence exponent as a bilinear form involving the difference of stress-energy tensors of the two branches of the superposition and an effective noise kernel constructed from graviton-dressed correlators of the environmental stress-energy tensor. They then specialize to Newtonian gravity and model the environment as a dilute nonrelativistic gas of finite wave packets, applying coarse-graining in space and time to compute the cumulative decoherence function analytically under controlled approximations.
\n\n
作者采用闭合时间路径（closed-time-path, CTP）形式，对与引力耦合的环境标量场进行积分，从而得到系统粒子的影响泛函。在非相对论和准静态极限下，他们将退相干指数表达为叠加态两个分支的应力-能量张量之差与一个有效噪声核的双线性形式，该噪声核由引力子传播子“dress”后的环境应力-能量张量关联函数构成。随后，他们将框架应用于牛顿引力情形，并将环境建模为有限波包组成的稀薄非相对论气体，在时空上进行粗粒化，并在可控近似下解析计算累积退相干函数。

Result: The dominant contribution to gravitational decoherence arises from long-range Newtonian interactions and accumulates logarithmically over a broad range of distances. However, under realistic physical conditions (e.g., laboratory or astrophysical environments), this gravitational decoherence rate remains subdominant compared to conventional collisional decoherence from direct particle scattering.
\n\n
引力退相干的主要贡献来自长程牛顿相互作用，并在大范围距离上以对数形式累积。然而，在现实物理条件下（如实验室或天体物理环境），这种引力退相干率相较于由粒子直接散射引起的常规碰撞退相干仍为次主导项。

Conclusion: Gravitational decoherence, while theoretically present due to coupling to environmental degrees of freedom via linearized gravity, is too weak to dominate over standard environmental decoherence mechanisms in practical scenarios. This supports the view that gravity alone—within this perturbative quantum field theory treatment—does not resolve the measurement problem but provides a quantifiable, albeit small, decoherence channel.
\n\n
引力退相干虽然通过线性化引力与环境自由度耦合而在理论上存在，但在实际场景中过于微弱，无法主导标准的环境退相干机制。这支持了如下观点：在此微扰量子场论处理框架下，引力本身不足以解决测量问题，但确实提供了一个可量化（尽管微小）的退相干通道。

Abstract: We formulate the gravitationally induced quantum decoherence of a massive object prepared in a spatial superposition. Starting from linearized gravity coupled to a massive system particle and an environmental scalar field, we derive a closed-time-path influence functional governing the reduced system dynamics. In the nonrelativistic and quasi-static regime, the decoherence exponent can be written as a bilinear functional of the difference of the system stress-energy tensors and an effective noise kernel obtained by dressing the environmental stress-energy tensor correlator with graviton propagators. We then apply this framework to the Newtonian long-range gravitational interaction and evaluate the resulting decoherence function for a dilute nonrelativistic gas modeled by finite wave packets and coarse-grained in time and space. By performing controlled approximations, we obtain analytic expressions for the cumulative decoherence function and show that the dominant contribution is accumulated logarithmically over a broad range of distances, while remaining subdominant to conventional collisional decoherence under realistic conditions.

</details>


### [3] [Remarks on causal decrease, omniscient foliations and null lines in spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04148)
*Alfredo Bautista*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper constructs a counterexample showing that future null lines can exist in strongly causal spacetimes with a past-omniscient timelike foliation and compact leaf space, contradicting prior claims. It introduces a new condition—causal decrease of the defining timelike vector field—which allows weakening the global causality assumption to distinguishing while still excluding future null lines. For Generalized Robertson–Walker (GRW) spacetimes, it provides sufficient conditions on the scale function to ensure this causal decrease property.
\n\n
本文构建了一个反例，表明在具有过去全知类时叶状结构（past-omniscient timelike foliation）和紧致叶空间的强因果时空中，未来类光线（future null lines）可能存在，从而反驳了先前关于其不存在的断言。文章引入了一个新条件——定义叶状结构的类时矢量场需满足“因果递减”（causally decreasing）——在此条件下，可将整体因果性要求从强因果性放宽至区分性（distinguishing），同时仍能排除未来类光线。对于广义Robertson–Walker（GRW）时空，文中给出了尺度函数（scale function）的充分条件，以确保该类时矢量场具有因果递减性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous literature claimed that future null lines cannot exist in strongly causal spacetimes admitting a past-omniscient timelike foliation with compact leaf space $Q$. The authors aim to challenge this assertion by constructing an explicit counterexample, thereby clarifying the precise causal and geometric conditions under which such pathological causal curves are excluded. This motivates the search for a refined hypothesis that restores the non-existence result under weaker causality assumptions.
\n\n
先前文献声称，在具有过去全知类时叶状结构且叶空间 $Q$ 紧致的强因果时空中，未来类光线上不可能存在。作者旨在通过构造一个明确的反例来挑战这一断言，从而厘清排除此类病理性因果曲线所需的精确因果与几何条件。这促使他们寻找一个更精细的假设，使得在更弱的因果性假设下仍能恢复“未来类光线不存在”的结论。

Method: The authors first construct a spacetime $(M, \mathbf{g})$ that is strongly causal, admits a past-omniscient timelike foliation $\mathcal{F}$ with compact leaf space, yet contains a future null line—thus serving as a counterexample. They then introduce the notion of a "causally decreasing" timelike vector field $X$ (defining $\mathcal{F}$), meaning that the function $p \mapsto g(X_p, V_p)$ is non-increasing along any future-directed causal curve, for any future-directed causal vector field $V$. Under this hypothesis, they prove that the causality condition can be weakened from strong causality to the distinguishing property while still precluding future null lines. Finally, for GRW spacetimes of the form $-dt^2 + f^2(t) h$, they analyze the natural timelike vector field $\partial_t$ and derive sufficient conditions on the scale function $f(t)$ (e.g., monotonicity or convexity properties) ensuring it is causally decreasing.
\n\n
作者首先构造一个时空 $(M, \mathbf{g})$，该时空是强因果的，具有过去全知类时叶状结构 $\mathcal{F}$ 且叶空间紧致，但包含一条未来类光线——从而构成反例。随后，他们引入“因果递减”类时矢量场 $X$（定义 $\mathcal{F}$）的概念：即对任意未来指向的因果矢量场 $V$，函数 $p \mapsto g(X_p, V_p)$ 沿任何未来指向的因果曲线非增。在此假设下，他们证明因果性条件可从强因果性弱化为区分性（distinguishing），同时仍能排除未来类光线。最后，针对形如 $-dt^2 + f^2(t) h$ 的广义Robertson–Walker（GRW）时空，他们分析自然类时矢量场 $\partial_t$，并推导出尺度函数 $f(t)$ 的充分条件（例如单调性或凸性），以确保其满足因果递减性。

Result: (1) A counterexample is provided showing that future null lines can exist in strongly causal spacetimes with a past-omniscient timelike foliation and compact leaf space, refuting a prior non-existence claim. (2) If the timelike vector field defining the foliation is causally decreasing, then the absence of future null lines holds under the weaker distinguishing causality condition. (3) In GRW spacetimes, if the scale function $f(t)$ satisfies $\\dot{f}(t) \\leq 0$ (or more generally, if $f$ is non-increasing), then the natural vector field $\\partial_t$ is causally decreasing, guaranteeing no future null lines.
\n\n
(1) 提供了一个反例，表明在具有过去全知类时叶状结构和紧致叶空间的强因果时空中，未来类光线可能存在，从而否定了先前关于其不存在的断言。(2) 若定义叶状结构的类时矢量场是因果递减的，则在更弱的区分性因果条件下，未来类光线仍不存在。(3) 在GRW时空中，若尺度函数 $f(t)$ 满足 $\\dot{f}(t) \\leq 0$（或更一般地，$f$ 非增），则自然矢量场 $\\partial_t$ 是因果递减的，从而保证不存在未来类光线。

Conclusion: The non-existence of future null lines in spacetimes with past-omniscient timelike foliations is not guaranteed by strong causality alone when the leaf space is compact. A key additional geometric condition—the causal decrease of the defining timelike vector field—is necessary to ensure this result under minimal causality assumptions. This insight refines our understanding of the interplay between foliation structure, causal properties, and the global geometry of spacetime, particularly in cosmological models like GRW spacetimes.
\n\n
在叶空间紧致的情况下，仅凭强因果性不足以保证具有过去全知类时叶状结构的时空中未来类光线不存在。要在此类时空中以最小因果性假设确保该结果，关键还需附加一个几何条件——即定义叶状结构的类时矢量场必须满足因果递减性。这一见解深化了我们对叶状结构、因果性质与时空整体几何之间相互作用的理解，尤其适用于GRW等宇宙学模型。

Abstract: This work provides a counterexample to the previously asserted non--existence of future null lines in strongly causal spacetimes $(M, \mathbf{g})$ foliated by a past--omniscient timelike foliation $\mathcal{F}$ with a compact leaf space $Q$. We introduce a new hypothesis requiring the timelike vector field that defines $\mathcal{F}$ to be causally decreasing. Under this condition, the causality requirement on $M$ can be relaxed to the distinguishing level. In the absence of this hypothesis, we demonstrate that more restrictive conditions, such as global hyperbolicity, are necessary to ensure the absence of future null lines. Finally, we derive sufficient conditions on the scale function of Generalized Robertson--Walker (GRW) spacetimes to ensure that the natural timelike vector field is causally decreasing, thereby guaranteeing the non--existence of future null lines.

</details>


### [4] [Shape of U: Measuring the Curvature of the Universe with Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04216)
*Arindam Sharma,Ish Gupta,Anuradha Gupta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper forecasts that next-generation gravitational wave (GW) observatories—specifically a network of two Cosmic Explorer detectors and the Einstein Telescope—can constrain the spatial curvature parameter $Ω_{\rm k}$ to $1σ$ uncertainty of $0.029$ using bright sirens from intermediate-mass binary black hole mergers (e.g., GW231123-like events). Binary neutron star bright sirens yield weaker constraints ($σ(Ω_{\rm k}) = 0.055$), and multiband observations with LISA or the Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna offer negligible improvement due to limited additional signal-to-noise ratio. These GW-based measurements provide an independent probe of cosmic curvature with systematics distinct from traditional cosmological methods.
\n\n
本文预测，下一代引力波（GW）探测器——特别是由两个Cosmic Explorer探测器和Einstein Telescope组成的网络——利用中等质量双黑洞并合（如类似GW231123的事件）作为“亮汽笛”（bright siren），可将空间曲率参数 $Ω_{\rm k}$ 的 $1σ$ 不确定度限制在 $0.029$。双中子星亮汽笛给出的约束较弱（$σ(Ω_{\rm k}) = 0.055$），而结合LISA或月基引力波天线（Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna）的多波段观测因信噪比增益有限，对宇宙学约束几乎没有改善。这类基于引力波的测量提供了与传统宇宙学方法系统误差不同的、独立且信息丰富的空间曲率探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need for independent, calibration-free probes of cosmic expansion and spatial curvature. Standard sirens—GWs from compact binary mergers—offer such a probe because their luminosity distance is directly inferred from waveform modeling without cosmic distance ladders. When paired with electromagnetic redshifts (“bright sirens”), they enable direct measurement of the Hubble diagram and thus constraints on $Ω_{\rm k}$ in non-flat $Λ$CDM. Intermediate-mass binary black holes (IMBHs) are promising because next-generation ground-based detectors can observe them to high redshifts, and AGN disk environments may produce detectable electromagnetic counterparts, making them viable bright sirens.
\n\n
动机源于对独立且无需校准的宇宙膨胀与空间曲率探针的需求。标准汽笛（standard siren）——来自致密双星并合的引力波——因其光度距离可直接从波形建模中推断，无需依赖宇宙距离阶梯，从而提供此类探针。当与电磁红移结合（即“亮汽笛”）时，可直接构建哈勃图，进而约束非平坦 $Λ$CDM 模型中的 $Ω_{\rm k}$。中等质量双黑洞（IMBH）具有潜力，因为下一代地面探测器可观测到高红移处的此类事件，且活动星系核（AGN）盘环境可能产生可探测的电磁对应体，使其成为可行的亮汽笛源。

Method: The authors employ Fisher matrix forecasting to estimate the precision with which future GW detector networks can constrain $Ω_{\rm k}$. They model bright siren populations based on intermediate-mass binary black hole mergers (inspired by GW231123) and binary neutron star mergers, assuming redshifts are provided by electromagnetic counterparts. The analysis includes a network of two Cosmic Explorer detectors and the Einstein Telescope. They also assess the impact of multiband GW observations by incorporating LISA and the proposed Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna, computing the combined signal-to-noise ratio across frequency bands to evaluate improvements in parameter estimation.
\n\n
作者采用Fisher矩阵方法预测未来引力波探测器网络对 $Ω_{\rm k}$ 的约束精度。他们基于中等质量双黑洞并合（以GW231123为参考）和双中子星并合构建亮汽笛种群模型，并假设红移由电磁对应体提供。分析涵盖由两个Cosmic Explorer探测器和Einstein Telescope组成的网络。此外，他们评估了多波段引力波观测的影响，通过纳入LISA和提议中的月基引力波天线（Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna），计算跨频段的联合信噪比，以评估参数估计精度的提升程度。

Result: The main result is that intermediate-mass binary black hole bright sirens observed by a network of two Cosmic Explorer detectors and Einstein Telescope can constrain $Ω_{\rm k}$ with $1σ$ uncertainty of $0.029$. In contrast, binary neutron star bright sirens yield a larger uncertainty of $0.055$. Multiband observations with LISA or the Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna do not significantly improve these constraints because the additional signal-to-noise ratio in the mHz band is modest for these sources. Thus, the dominant constraining power comes from ground-based detectors observing the merger/ringdown phase.
\n\n
主要结果是：由两个Cosmic Explorer探测器和Einstein Telescope组成的网络观测到的中等质量双黑洞亮汽笛可将 $Ω_{\rm k}$ 的 $1σ$ 不确定度限制在 $0.029$；相比之下，双中子星亮汽笛的不确定度更大，为 $0.055$。LISA或月基引力波天线的多波段观测并未显著改善这些约束，因为这些源在毫赫兹波段带来的额外信噪比较小。因此，约束能力主要来自地面探测器对并合/铃宕阶段的观测。

Conclusion: Bright sirens from intermediate-mass binary black holes and binary neutron stars, as observed by next-generation ground-based GW detectors, provide a complementary and independent probe of spatial curvature $Ω_{\rm k}$ in $Λ$CDM cosmology. Their systematics differ fundamentally from those of CMB, BAO, or supernova observations, offering a valuable cross-check. While multiband GW astronomy is promising for other applications, it offers minimal gains for $Ω_{\rm k}$ constraints with the considered source populations. This work underscores the cosmological potential of IMBH mergers as bright sirens.
\n\n
由下一代地面引力波探测器观测到的中等质量双黑洞和双中子星亮汽笛，为 $Λ$CDM 宇宙学中的空间曲率 $Ω_{\rm k}$ 提供了一个互补且独立的探针。其系统误差与CMB、BAO或超新星观测有本质区别，可提供宝贵的交叉验证。尽管多波段引力波天文学在其他应用中前景广阔，但对于本文考虑的源种群，其对 $Ω_{\rm k}$ 约束的增益微乎其微。本工作凸显了中等质量黑洞并合作为亮汽笛在宇宙学中的潜力。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) from compact binary mergers are standard sirens that can measure distances across the Universe without external calibrators. When an electromagnetic counterpart enables an independent redshift measurement, such "bright sirens" can be used to probe the expansion history of the Universe and constrain cosmological models. In this work, we investigate the ability of future GW observatories to measure the spatial curvature parameter, $Ω_{\rm k}$, in a non-flat $Λ$CDM cosmology. We focus on intermediate-mass binary black hole mergers (with masses similar to GW231123) as bright siren sources, motivated by their detectability to high redshifts with next-generation ground-based detectors and by the possibility that mergers in active galactic nucleus disks may produce electromagnetic counterparts. Using Fisher matrix forecasts, we find that a network consisting of two Cosmic Explorer detectors and Einstein Telescope can constrain $Ω_{\rm k}$ to a $1σ$ uncertainty of $0.029$ with these bright sirens. We further show that multiband observations with LISA or the Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna do not significantly improve these cosmological constraints, because the additional signal-to-noise ratios accumulated in their bands are modest. Further, a population of binary neutron stars as bright sirens provides substantially broader constraints on $Ω_{\rm k}$, with $1σ$ error of $0.055$. Our results show that bright intermediate-mass binary black hole and binary neutron star mergers observed with next-generation GW detectors together can provide an independent and informative probe of spatial curvature, with systematics distinct from those of other cosmological observations.

</details>


### [5] [Real Part Emergence in Purely Imaginary Quasinormal Modes in Perturbed de Sitter Braneworlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04424)
*Hai-Long Jia,Wen-Di Guo,Yun-Tao Gu,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies the stability of gravitational perturbations on a thick de Sitter brane in braneworld models with infinite extra dimensions. It finds that while unperturbed de Sitter branes support purely imaginary quasinormal modes (non-oscillatory decay), introducing brane-localized perturbations induces complex quasinormal frequencies, leading to transient oscillatory behavior in the time-domain signal. These early-time oscillations may affect observational inference of the brane cosmological constant.
\n\n

Abstract: For braneworlds with infinite extra dimensions, an analysis of the stability of the characteristic spectrum is essential for understanding their dynamical properties. Unlike the flat brane case, the de Sitter brane features purely imaginary quasinormal frequencies, corresponding to time-domain signals that decay without oscillation. Abstract: We present IMRPhenomXHM_NSBH and SEOBNRv5HM_ROM_NRTidalv3_NSBH, the first two frequency-domain models for gravitational-wave signals from quasi-circular, aligned-spin neutron star--black hole (NSBH) binaries including higher-order modes beyond the dominant quadrupole. This work aims to fill that gap by rigorously incorporating FLRW geometry into the statistical description of apparent transverse velocities.

Method: The authors adopt a statistical framework assuming isotropic source orientations and general intrinsic peculiar velocity distributions. They compute the apparent angular velocity $\\mu = v_{\\rm app}/D_{\\rm com}(z)$, where $v_{\\rm app}$ is the apparent transverse velocity and $D_{\\rm com}(z)$ is the comoving distance at redshift $z$. PDFs of $\\mu$ are derived analytically under minimal assumptions. Statistical observables (e.g., mean, variance, quantiles) are constructed and shown to depend explicitly on $Ω_{Λ,0}$ and $H_0$. By combining measurements across multiple redshifts, they construct joint likelihoods to constrain the $(Ω_{Λ,0}, H_0)$ plane and break degeneracies.

The method yields correlated constraints in the $(Ω_{Λ,0}, H_0)$ plane, demonstrating that apparent superluminal motion can serve as a viable kinematic probe of cosmic expansion history.

所推导的概率密度函数及其统计矩对$Ω_{Λ,0}$和$H_0$均表现出明显敏感性。当仅使用单个红移区间的观测数据时，两参数间存在强简并；但结合多个红移的观测后，该简并显著减弱。该方法在$(Ω_{Λ,0}, H_0)$平面上给出相关约束，表明视超光速运动可作为探测宇宙膨胀历史的有效运动学探针。

Conclusion: Apparent superluminal motion, when treated with proper cosmological corrections via comoving distance at emission time, provides a novel and complementary kinematic test of the standard cosmological model. Abstract: We employ a statistical approach to study apparent superluminal motion of luminous sources in an expanding flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker universe, explicitly incorporating cosmological effects through the comoving distance at the emission time. Probability Density Functions (PDFs) of the apparent angular velocity are derived under minimal assumptions regarding source orientations and intrinsic peculiar velocity distributions. We show that the apparent angular velocity distributions and their associated statistical observables are sensitive to cosmological parameters $Ω_{Λ,0}$ and the Hubble parameter $H_0$. Using suitably defined observables, we construct correlated constraints in the $(Ω_{Λ,0}, H_0)$ parameter space and demonstrate that combining measurements at different redshifts effectively breaks the resulting degeneracy. Apparent superluminal motion thus provides a complementary kinematic consistency test for cosmological models.

*Jacopo Tissino,Filippo Santoliquido,Francesco Iacovelli,Ulyana Dupletsa,Tito Dal Canton,Matteo Ballelli,Ansh Chopra,Luis Enrique Espinosa Castro,Laura Pezzella,Matteo Schulz,Izumi Takimoto Schmiegelow,Jan Harms*

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that by optimizing the choice of reference frame origin—specifically selecting a point within the Solar System that minimizes timing uncertainty for long-duration gravitational wave (GW) signals—the Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna (LGWA) can achieve significantly improved parameter estimation with an order-of-magnitude reduction in sampling time. Method: The authors develop a post-processing procedure to identify the optimal origin within the Solar System that minimizes the timing residual variance for a given GW signal. They adopt a frame comoving with the SSB but translate its spatial origin to this optimal point. They compare different timing parametrizations (e.g., merger time vs. time at coalescence reference frequency) and assess their impact on posterior uncertainties via Bayesian inference. They validate their approach using simulated injections of the binary black hole event GW250114 into LGWA noise, and test the analytical approximation by Wen and Chen relating sky localization area to the detector’s orbital trajectory area.

Conclusion: Parameter inference for long-duration deci-Hertz GW signals with LGWA is fundamentally a geometrical problem governed by the interplay of detector motion, reference-frame choice, and signal evolution. Abstract: The Lunar Gravitational Wave Antenna (LGWA) is a planned gravitational wave detector on the Moon, targeting the deci-Hertz band and expected to deliver breakthrough discoveries across several science cases, including the Moon's interior structure and astrophysics. In this work, we show that adopting a frame comoving with the Solar System barycenter (SSB), but with its origin at a location that minimizes timing uncertainty, reduces the sampling time by an order of magnitude. We present a systematic post-processing procedure to identify the optimal origin within the Solar System for any given signal. We explore alternative timing parametrizations beyond the merger time, and find that they have only a minor impact on parameter uncertainties. Abstract: To model gravitational waveforms from extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) for the upcoming LISA space mission, gravitational self-force calculations are needed to second order in perturbation theory. However, to date these calculations have only been attempted for the simplest case of circular orbits in Schwarzschild spacetime. We perform an $m$-mode separation of variables, add null ("$vtu$") slicing in horizon-penetrating coordinates, and solve the resulting elliptic PDEs using high-order discontinuous Galerkin discretization, adaptive mesh-refinement, and an iterative Krylov-type linear solver with parallelizable multigrid-Schwarz preconditioning. Abstract: Charged dilaton black holes with a positive cosmological constant provide a useful arena in which to test how scalar hair modifies semiclassical physics in a spacetime with two Killing horizons. The Gao--Zhang solution realizes such a geometry in Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton theory by replacing a single Liouville potential, which is insufficient for asymptotically de Sitter boundary conditions, by a three-Liouville dilaton potential. The dilaton changes the areal radius, entropy, photon-sphere condition, and greybody problem, while the de Sitter normalization ambiguity changes radiation estimates by powers of the redshift factor. For representative parameters, the Bousso--Hawking prescription can enhance a Stefan--Boltzmann estimate of the black-hole power by one to two orders of magnitude relative to the unnormalized surface-gravity prescription, whereas the entropy-sum effective temperature can suppress the same estimate. These results identify a concrete gap in the thermodynamics of dilaton--de Sitter black holes and provide a roadmap for a full greybody-factor calculation.

Abstract: Measuring the redshift evolution of star formation rate density is crucial in understanding the origin and evolution of galaxies and large scale structure in the universe. The authors aim to extend SBP methodology to handle these singular geometries robustly while maintaining high-order accuracy and allowing grid points at the origin.

在曲线坐标（尤其是球坐标）中对双曲型偏微分方程进行数值模拟时，坐标奇点（例如 $r=0$ 处）会破坏标准有限差分格式的稳定性。传统的 Summation-by-Parts (SBP) 算子虽可通过离散分部积分类比保证能量稳定性，但在奇点附近通常失效。作者旨在将 SBP 方法推广至此类奇异几何情形，在保持高阶精度的同时允许网格点位于原点，从而实现稳健的数值处理。

Method: The authors generalize the SBP framework to spherical coordinates by constructing discrete gradient and divergence operators that satisfy a discrete version of the integration-by-parts identity, even in the presence of $1/r^p$ singularities at the origin. They place a grid point directly at $r=0$ and design stencils that respect the underlying geometry and symmetry. Operators are explicitly constructed up to sixth-order accuracy. Additionally, they review alternative SBP constructions that avoid placing a point exactly at the origin ("straddling" it). Spectral analysis and convergence tests are performed to assess operator quality.
\n\n
作者通过构建离散梯度与散度算子，将 SBP 框架推广至球坐标系，使其即使在原点存在 $1/r^p$ 奇异性的情况下仍满足分部积分恒等式的离散形式。他们在 $r=0$ 处直接设置网格点，并设计符合底层几何与对称性的差分模板。显式构造了最高六阶精度的算子。此外，还回顾了另一种不将网格点精确置于原点而是“跨越”原点的 SBP 构造方法。通过谱半径分析和收敛性测试评估算子性能。

Result: The constructed SBP operators achieve design orders of accuracy (up to sixth order), exhibit favorable spectral properties (bounded spectral radii), and yield stable, non-oscillatory evolutions for the scalar wave equation in spherical symmetry. Placing a grid point at the origin is shown to be compatible with energy stability when using the new operators. Comparisons demonstrate improved robustness over naive finite difference schemes near the coordinate singularity.
\n\n
所构造的 SBP 算子达到了设计精度（最高六阶），具有良好的谱性质（谱半径有界），并在球对称标量波动方程的演化中表现出稳定、无振荡的行为。研究表明，使用新算子时在原点放置网格点与能量稳定性兼容。与朴素有限差分格式相比，新方法在坐标奇点附近展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: The paper successfully extends the SBP formalism to handle coordinate singularities in spherical geometries by constructing high-order, energy-stable finite difference operators that respect the continuous integration-by-parts structure. This enables robust and accurate simulations of hyperbolic systems (e.g., wave equations, potentially GR) in spherical coordinates with a grid point at the origin—a configuration often desirable for symmetry and boundary condition implementation.
\n\n
本文成功将 SBP 形式体系扩展至处理球坐标中的坐标奇点问题，通过构造高阶、能量稳定的有限差分算子，保留了连续分部积分结构。这使得在球坐标下对双曲系统（如波动方程，甚至广义相对论）进行稳健且精确的数值模拟成为可能，尤其支持在原点设置网格点——这一配置在对称性和边界条件实施中通常更为理想。

Abstract: Formulating stable numerical methods for hyperbolic systems in curvilinear coordinate with singularities, e.g. spherical coordinates, is complicated by the presence of these singularities. We present a method for constructing high-order accurate, energy-stable finite difference operators satisfying the Summation-by-Parts (SBP) property on spherical domains, extending ideas presented by [C. Gundlach, J. M. Martín-García, and D. Garfinkle, CQG 30, 145003 (2013)]. We define discrete gradient and divergence operators that mirror the continuous integration-by-parts principle, even though there is a $1/r^p$ coordinate singularity present at the origin. We explicitly construct such operators up to order six. Our operators place a grid point directly on the origin. We also review how to construct stable SBP operators that straddle the origin. We analyze the accuracy and spectral radii of these operators, and we show example evolutions of the scalar wave equation to demonstrate the advantages of such operators.

</details>

### [15] [Coarse graining from within: Wilson-Fisher universality on $S^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04085)
*Alfio M. Bonanno,Renata Ferrero,Giovanni Oglialoro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper proposes a covariant, momentum-free Wilsonian renormalization group (RG) framework on curved spaces by using a spectral cutoff based on eigenvalues of the covariant Laplacian, demonstrating its validity through recovery of flat-space RG in the large-radius limit and reproduction of Wilson-Fisher fixed point physics on $S^3$.
\n\n
本文提出了一种在弯曲空间上协变且无需动量的Wilsonian重整化群（RG）框架，通过基于协变Laplace算子本征值的谱截断实现，并通过在大半径极限下恢复平直空间RG以及在$S^3$上重现Wilson-Fisher不动点物理来验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard Wilsonian RG relies on momentum-shell integrations, which lack diffeomorphism invariance on curved manifolds like $S^3$, making it ill-suited for quantum field theory in nontrivial geometries or quantum gravity contexts. A manifestly covariant RG formalism is needed that does not depend on a preferred momentum basis.
\n\n
标准的Wilsonian RG依赖于动量壳层积分，但在如$S^3$这样的弯曲流形上缺乏微分同胚不变性，因此不适用于非平凡几何中的量子场论或量子引力背景。亟需一种不依赖于特定动量基的显式协变RG形式体系。

Method: The authors replace momentum shells with a spectral cutoff defined by the eigenvalues $\\lambda_\\ell = \\ell(\\ell+2)$ of the covariant Laplacian $-\\nabla^2$ on $S^3$, where $\\ell$ labels spherical harmonics. The RG scale is tied to the physical system size in RG units, and the flow is generated by integrating out modes above a cutoff $\\Lambda$, yielding a trace over spherical harmonic modes. The resulting beta functions are computed without reference to momentum space.
\n\n
作者用协变Laplace算子$-\\nabla^2$在$S^3$上的本征值$\\lambda_\\ell = \\ell(\\ell+2)$所定义的谱截断取代动量壳层，其中$\\ell$标记球谐函数。RG标度与RG单位下的物理系统尺寸相关联，通过积分掉高于截断$\\Lambda$的模式生成流，从而得到对球谐模式的迹。所得beta函数无需引用动量空间即可计算。

Result: The spectral RG flow exactly reproduces the flat-space Wilsonian RG in the limit of large sphere radius $R \\to \\infty$. On finite $S^3$, it yields a non-Gaussian Wilson-Fisher fixed point with one relevant direction, and critical exponents that deviate only weakly from their flat-space values, confirming universality persists despite the compact spectrum.
\n\n
该谱RG流在球半径$R \\to \\infty$的大极限下精确复现了平直空间的Wilsonian RG。在有限$S^3$上，它产生一个具有单一相关方向的非高斯Wilson-Fisher不动点，其临界指数仅轻微偏离平直空间值，证实了尽管存在紧致谱，普适性仍然成立。

Conclusion: A fully covariant, momentum-free formulation of Wilsonian RG is viable on compact curved spaces using spectral decomposition of the Laplacian. This approach preserves key features of standard RG—such as fixed points and universality—while being intrinsically geometric, opening pathways for RG applications in quantum gravity and cosmology.
\n\n
利用Laplace算子的谱分解，在紧致弯曲空间上可构建完全协变且无需动量的Wilsonian RG表述。该方法保留了标准RG的关键特征（如不动点和普适性），同时具有内蕴几何性质，为RG在量子引力和宇宙学中的应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Wilsonian renormalization is usually formulated in momentum space, but on curved backgrounds momentum shells have no invariant meaning. We replace them by an intrinsic spectral cutoff, ordering modes by the covariant Laplacian and setting the cutoff resolution by the system size in renormalization group (RG) units. For a scalar field on $S^3$, this yields a covariant, momentum-free RG flow whose trace is an exact sum over spherical harmonics. The standard flat-space flow is recovered when the sphere is large compared with the coarse-graining scale. As a nontrivial test, the compact spectral flow realizes Wilson-Fisher universality without momentum shells: the interacting fixed point survives at finite resolution, has one relevant direction, and approaches its flat-space counterpart smoothly, with critical exponents only weakly affected by the compact spectrum.

</details>

TL;DR: The paper proposes an algorithmic method to construct closed string trajectories by using a single open string as a seed and dressing it with symplectic algebra generators acting on both left- and right-moving sectors, bypassing the inefficiency of the double copy approach. Physical closed string states are identified by solving Diophantine-like recursion relations derived via Howe duality.

本文提出了一种算法化方法，通过将单个开弦作为种子，并用作用于左右移动扇区的辛代数（symplectic algebra）生成元对其进行“dress”，从而构建闭弦轨迹，绕过了双拷贝（double copy）方法的低效性。借助Howe对偶性导出的类丢番图（Diophantine-like）递归关系，可从中筛选出物理的闭弦态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The standard "double copy" construction—gluing two open string spectra to form closed strings—is combinatorially inefficient for generating full closed string trajectories. \n\n
标准的“双拷贝”（double copy）构造——将两个开弦谱拼接形成闭弦——在生成完整闭弦轨迹时存在组合复杂性导致的低效问题。作者旨在发展一种更系统且计算上可行的框架，利用代数结构（特别是辛代数和Howe对偶性），从极简的开弦输入直接生成物理的闭弦态。

Method: The method starts with a single physical open string trajectory as a seed. This seed is then acted upon by generators of a symplectic algebra $\\mathfrak{sp}(2n)$ that simultaneously transform both the left- and right-moving sectors. Using Howe duality—a duality between representations of symplectic and orthogonal groups—the resulting dressed states are interpreted as candidates for closed string trajectories. The physical subset is isolated by imposing constraints in the form of Diophantine-like recursion relations derived from consistency conditions (e.g., level-matching and Virasoro constraints).
\n\n
该方法以一条物理的开弦轨迹作为种子出发，随后由辛代数 $\\mathfrak{sp}(2n)$ 的生成元同时作用于左右移动扇区对其进行“dress”。借助Howe对偶性（辛群与正交群表示之间的对偶性），所得的dress态被解释为闭弦轨迹的候选。通过施加一致性条件（如能级匹配和Virasoro约束）导出的类丢番图递归关系，从中筛选出物理子集。

Result: The authors demonstrate that their algorithm successfully generates closed string trajectory candidates and illustrate the extraction of physical states through explicit examples involving Diophantine-like recursion relations. The approach avoids the exponential complexity of brute-force double copy and provides a representation-theoretic pathway grounded in Howe duality and symplectic symmetry.
\n\n
作者展示了其算法能成功生成闭弦轨迹候选，并通过涉及类丢番图递归关系的具体例子说明了如何提取物理态。该方法避免了暴力双拷贝带来的指数级复杂性，提供了一条基于Howe对偶性和辛对称性的表示论路径。

Conclusion: The proposed symplectic dressing method, combined with Howe duality, offers a novel and efficient alternative to the double copy for constructing closed string spectra. It reveals a deep connection between string theory and representation theory, opening avenues for systematic exploration of closed string state spaces using algebraic techniques.
\n\n
所提出的辛代数dress方法结合Howe对偶性，为构建闭弦谱提供了一种新颖且高效的双拷贝替代方案。它揭示了弦论与表示论之间的深刻联系，为利用代数技术系统探索闭弦态空间开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Based on an efficient technology for the excavation of entire open string trajectories of physical states, we propose an algorithmic method of constructing the largely unknown closed string trajectories. Due to combinatorial complexity, the "double copy" of open strings is limited in efficiency as a means of building closed string trajectories. We bypass this technical difficulty by employing one open string as the fundamental seed and then dressing it by a suitable selection of generators of a symplectic algebra that acts on both the left and the right sector. By applying Howe duality, the dressed seeds amount to closed string trajectory candidates, finding the physical subset of which is possible by solving systems of equations involving Diophantine-like recursion relations, which we also illustrate with examples.

</details>

### [18] [Multi-entropy in random tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04470)
*Miao Hu,Simon Lin,Ion Nechita*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper investigates Rényi multi-entropies $S^{(q)}_n$ in Random Tensor Network (RTN) states in the large bond-dimension limit. For $n=2$, it proves that multi-entropies are given by minimal multiway cuts, generalizing the known bipartite minimal-cut prescription to arbitrary multipartite settings, and characterizes degeneracies via compatible families of cuts. For integer $n>2$, it disproves the minimal multiway cut conjecture with explicit counterexamples, impacting our understanding of multipartite entanglement in RTNs and holography.
\n\n
本文研究了大键维极限下随机张量网络（Random Tensor Network, RTN）态中的Rényi多体熵 $S^{(q)}_n$。对于 $n=2$，证明了多体熵由网络中的最小多路割（minimal multiway cuts）决定，将已知的二分纠缠的最小割描述自然推广至任意多方情形，并通过相容的最小割族刻画了简并情形。对于整数 $n>2$，通过显式反例否定了最小多路割猜想，这对理解RTN及全息对偶中的多体纠缠结构具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from extending the well-established connection between bipartite entanglement entropy and minimal cuts in tensor networks—especially in the context of holography via the Ryu–Takayanagi formula—to multipartite settings using Rényi entropies. Understanding how multipartite entanglement is geometrized in RTNs can shed light on quantum gravity and holographic entanglement structures beyond bipartite cases.
\n\n
动机源于将张量网络中二分纠缠熵与最小割之间已确立的联系（尤其在全息对偶中通过Ryu–Takayanagi公式）推广到使用Rényi熵的多方情形。理解RTN中多体纠缠如何被几何化，有助于揭示超越二分情形的量子引力和全息纠缠结构。

Method: The authors analyze Rényi multi-entropies $S^{(q)}_n$ in RTN states in the large bond-dimension limit using diagrammatic and combinatorial techniques. For $n=2$, they map the entropy calculation to a graph-theoretic problem of finding minimal multiway cuts separating $q$ boundary regions. They introduce the notion of compatible families of minimal cuts to handle degeneracy. For $n>2$, they construct explicit counterexamples—involving a single random tensor and isometric tiling networks—where the entropy does not correspond to any minimal multiway cut, thereby falsifying the conjecture for higher Rényi indices.
\n\n
作者在大键维极限下，利用图示法和组合方法分析RTN态中的Rényi多体熵 $S^{(q)}_n$。对于 $n=2$，他们将熵的计算映射为图论中的最小多路割问题，即分离 $q$ 个边界区域的最小割。他们引入“相容最小割族”的概念处理简并情形。对于 $n>2$，他们构造了显式反例——包括单个随机张量和等距铺砌网络——其中熵不对应于任何最小多路割，从而证伪了高阶Rényi指数下的该猜想。

Result: For Rényi index $n=2$, the multi-entropy $S^{(q)}_2$ equals the logarithm of the bond dimension times the size of the minimal multiway cut, generalizing the bipartite result. In degenerate cases, all minimizers are described by compatible families of cuts, and a criterion is given for when these arise from ordinary partitions. For integer $n>2$, explicit RTN configurations are provided where $S^{(q)}_n$ deviates from the minimal multiway cut prediction, disproving the conjecture in general.

对于Rényi指数 $n=2$，多体熵 $S^{(q)}_2$ 等于键维对数乘以最小多路割的大小，推广了二分情形的结果。在简并情形下，所有极小化解由相容割族描述，并给出了这些解源自普通割划分的判据。对于整数 $n>2$，文中给出了具体的RTN构型，其中 $S^{(q)}_n$ 偏离最小多路割预言，从而一般性地否定了该猜想。

Conclusion: The minimal cut prescription for entanglement entropy in RTNs holds precisely for $n=2$ and extends naturally to multipartite settings via minimal multiway cuts. However, this geometric picture breaks down for higher Rényi indices ($n>2$), indicating that multipartite entanglement structure in quantum many-body systems and holography is more nuanced than a simple cut-based geometric rule. This highlights a fundamental distinction between second Rényi entropy and higher moments in encoding entanglement geometry.
\n\n
RTN中纠缠熵的最小割描述恰好在 $n=2$ 时成立，并通过最小多路割自然推广至多方情形。然而，这一几何图像在更高Rényi指数（$n>2$）下失效，表明量子多体系统及全息对偶中的多体纠缠结构比简单的基于割的几何规则更为精细。这凸显了第二Rényi熵与高阶矩在编码纠缠几何时的根本差异。

Abstract: We study the evaluation of Rényi multi-entropies $S^{(q)}_n$ in Random Tensor Network (RTN) states in the large bond-dimension limit. For the case of Rényi index $n=2$ and arbitrary number of parties $q$, we prove that that multi-entropies are determined by minimal multiway cuts through the network. When the minimal multiway cut is degenerate, we characterize the full minimizer set via compatible families of minimal cuts and give a criterion for all minimizers to come from ordinary cut partitions. For $n=2$, this gives a natural generalization of the minimal cut description of bipartite entanglement to multipartite systems with arbitrarily many parties. For the case of integer $n>2$, we show that the minimal multiway cut conjecture is in general \emph{not true} by providing explicit counter examples for both the single random tensor and for the network built from isometric tilings. We discuss the implication for our results on the multipartite entanglement structures in RTN and holography.

</details>

动机源于理解非超对称弦构造的宇宙学含义的需求，这类构造通常产生指数型标量势。这些模型常涉及紧致化在最大对称空间上的额外维度，从而导出具有空间曲率（$k$）和内空间曲率（$k'$）的有效四维宇宙学。目前尚缺乏对此类动力学系统的系统性分析，尤其是在多种竞争效应（曲率项与势能项）共存时的普适行为。

Result: The paper provides a semi-quantitative phase portrait of the cosmological dynamics for various $(k, k')$ combinations. In the physically relevant case of flat spatial slices ($k=0$), it identifies distinct asymptotic regimes—such as potential-dominated, curvature-dominated, or scaling solutions—and characterizes their stability and singularity structure (e.g., big bang, big crunch, or bouncing behaviors).   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how features of closed universes—such as the emergence of a type II$_1$ von Neumann algebra, state indistinguishability under simple observables, and the role of wormholes in gravitational path integrals—can arise from concrete holographic CFT constructions. Method: The authors start with tensor product states constructed from a finite-dimensional low-energy subspace of the seed CFT in a symmetric orbifold theory at large $N$. They analyze the structure of the ungauged Hilbert space, identifying superselection sectors labeled by occupation number distributions. They study entanglement and operator algebras in the maximally entropic sector, showing it forms a hyperfinite type II$_1$ von Neumann algebra. Then, they impose the $S_N$ orbifold gauge constraint and compute the scaling of the physical Hilbert space dimension with $N$. Finally, they compare CFT results with gravitational path integral predictions, examining why the Hartle-Hawking saddle fails and how coupling to external observers restores agreement.
\n\n
作者从对称轨道折叠理论中种子CFT的有限维低能子空间出发，构建张量积态，并在大 $N$ 极限下分析未规范化的希尔伯特空间结构，识别出由占据数分布标记的超选择子空间。他们研究最大熵子空间中的纠缠性质与算符代数，证明其构成一个超有限型 II$_1$ 冯·诺依曼代数。随后施加 $S_N$ 轨道折叠规范约束，并计算物理希尔伯特空间维度随 $N$ 的标度行为。最后，他们将CFT结果与引力路径积分预言进行对比，阐明为何Hartle-Hawking鞍点近似失效，以及如何通过将CFT自由度与外部观测者耦合来恢复与半经典引力的一致性。

Result: Key results include: (1) decomposition of the ungauged large $N$ Hilbert space into exponentially large superselection sectors; (2) identification of the maximally entropic sector as exhibiting closed-universe-like properties—state indistinguishability and a type II$_1$ von Neumann algebra; (3) after $S_N$ gauging, physical Hilbert space dimension grows only polynomially in $N$, rendering each sector one-dimensional in the large $N$ limit; (4) this matches the qualitative behavior from gravitational path integrals with wormholes; (5) the standard Hartle-Hawking approximation fails to capture CFT physics, but agreement is restored when CFT is coupled to external observer degrees of freedom.

主要结果包括：(1) 未规范化的大型 $N$ 希尔伯特空间分解为指数级大的超选择子空间；(2) 最大熵子空间展现出闭合宇宙的典型特征——态的不可区分性及II$_1$型冯·诺依曼代数结构；(3) 施加 $S_N$ 规范约束后，物理希尔伯特空间维度仅随 $N$ 多项式增长，导致在大 $N$ 极限下各子空间退化为一维；(4) 这与包含虫洞的引力路径积分所预言的定性行为一致；(5) 标准Hartle-Hawking近似无法复现CFT结果，但当CFT与外部观测者自由度耦合时，可重建与半经典引力路径积分的吻合。

Abstract: We identify closed universe sectors in large $N$ symmetric orbifold CFTs with holographic duals. Starting from tensor product states built out of a finite dimensional low energy subspace of the seed theory, we show that the large $N$ Hilbert space decomposes into superselection sectors labeled by occupation number distributions. Before imposing the orbifold gauge constraint, these sectors have exponentially large dimensions, and the maximally entropic sector dominates the ungauged Hilbert space. Consequently, each superselection sector after imposing the constraint is one dimensional in this limit. This reproduces the qualitative behavior suggested by gravitational path integral calculations with wormholes. We then show why, in this setup, the Hartle-Hawking type semiclassical approximation for the dominant closed universe fails to reproduce the CFT results. Nevertheless, the dominant saddle point approximation for gravitational path integral calculation is reconstructed once the CFT degrees of freedom are coupled to external observer degrees of freedom.

Method: The authors analyze the field equations of the self-dual higher spin theory in 4d under the ansatz that the spin-2 sector yields an AdS$_4$ geometry while the global symmetry is reduced to 3d Poincaré. They impose consistency conditions on the higher-spin master fields and solve the unfolded equations. By requiring that only specific fields acquire nontrivial vacuum expectation values while others remain trivial (i.e., correspond to constant conserved currents on the boundary and lack dynamical gauge potentials in the bulk), they systematically identify admissible solutions.

作者在自旋-2部分给出AdS$_4$几何、而整体对称性降至3维Poincaré的假设下，分析4维自对偶高自旋理论的场方程。他们对高自旋主场施加一致性条件并求解展开（unfolded）方程。通过要求仅特定场获得非平凡真空期望值，而其余场保持平凡（即对应于边界上的常数守恒流且在体中无动力学规范势），系统地识别出容许的解。

分析表明，唯一能将AdS$_4$等距群破缺至3维Poincaré的标量真空是一个双参数族。此外，作者构造了一族新解：除该标量外，一个自旋-$1/2$费米子纯粹沿Poincaré坐标中的径向传播。所有高自旋场（$S \geq 1$，不包括引力子和该费米子）均为平凡场：其体规范场为零，边界对偶为常数守恒流。

Abstract: In this paper we study the self-dual higher spin theory in $4d$ recently proposed in arXiv:2209.01966. The typical vacuum of higher spin theories is an empty AdS space-time. Abstract: Anomalous transport in equilibrium fermionic fluids chirally coupled to background Weyl-type nonmetricity is studied. The constitutive relation of the axial-vector current is evaluated from the equilibrium partition function obtained using transgression techniques, showing the existence of nonmetricity-mediated chiral separation effects driven by the fluid's vorticity and the Weyl magnetic field. A second nonminimal coupling of fermionic matter to metric-affine geometries proposed in the literature is also discussed.

Abstract: We revisit the path integral quantization of the null, or tensionless, bosonic string from the viewpoint that all local gauge symmetries of the Carrollian worldsheet must be gauge fixed before the quantum theory is defined. In the tensile-string construction the $bc$ ghosts are the Faddeev-Popov determinant for fixing $\mathrm{Diff}\times\mathrm{Weyl}$. The result changes the BRST complex and the anomaly problem: the usual $D=26$ check based only on the old BMS $bc$ ghosts is a partially gauge-fixed calculation, while the Carroll-Weyl covariant quantum theory must include the $s,b^s$ sector.

Abstract: It is well known that the algebra $o(2,4)$ generates the conformal group, but it can also be used to define some variants of the Yang model of noncommutative geometry on a curved spacetime. Starting from these examples, we construct a new physical model based on $o(2,4)$, that can be interpreted as a generalization of the Heisenberg algebra on phase space, with flat positions and momenta, but nontrivial commutation relations between positions and momenta and with the Planck constant promoted to an operator.

We determine these coefficients through two independent computations: an evaluation of the twisted partition function on the two-sphere in the high-temperature limit, and a direct path-integral computation on a generic weakly curved background. The two methods yield consistent results, providing a non-trivial check of the thermal effective action framework.

### [30] [Fermionic Kaluza-Klein mode mixing in braneworlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05086)
*Chun-E Fu,Wen-Xuan Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper studies fermionic Kaluza-Klein (KK) mode mixing in thick braneworld scenarios under generic background perturbations. It shows that spatially varying perturbations induce off-diagonal couplings in the 4D effective mass matrix, breaking the orthogonality of unperturbed KK modes. An exact Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) of the full Dirac mass matrix is used to obtain true physical eigenstates, revealing structured corrections to masses and wavefunctions. Parity properties of perturbations dictate whether Z2 symmetry is preserved or broken, with parity-even perturbations causing spatial polarization that activates previously "dark" KK modes by shifting their wavefunctions toward the brane.
\n\n
本文研究了在厚膜世界（thick braneworld）模型中，费米子Kaluza-Klein（KK）模在一般背景微扰下的混合效应。研究表明，沿额外维度具有非平凡空间分布的微扰会在四维有效质量矩阵中引入非对角耦合，破坏未微扰KK模的正交性。作者通过对完整的Dirac质量矩阵进行精确的奇异值分解（Singular Value Decomposition, SVD）来获得真实的物理本征态，揭示了质量与波函数受到的小但高度结构化的修正。微扰算符的宇称性质决定了Z2空间对称性是否被保持：奇宇称微扰仅引起同宇称混合而保持Z2对称性，而偶宇称微扰则导致跨宇称混合，破坏Z2对称性，使KK概率密度发生显著空间极化，从而将原本“暗”的KK模激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the limitation of standard Kaluza-Klein reduction in braneworld models: while static, isolated backgrounds admit a complete orthogonal basis of KK eigenmodes via a Schrödinger-like equation, this basis fails when generic spatially dependent perturbations are introduced. Such perturbations—ubiquitous in realistic cosmological or gravitational settings—break the simple diagonal structure of the 4D effective fermion mass matrix, invalidating the assumption that unperturbed KK modes remain physical eigenstates. A rigorous treatment preserving the 5D chiral structure is needed to identify the true physical spectrum and understand phenomenological consequences like the activation of otherwise invisible ("dark") KK states.
\n\n
动机源于膜世界模型中标准Kaluza-Klein约化方法的局限性：尽管静态孤立背景可通过类薛定谔方程得到一组完备正交的KK本征模基，但当引入具有空间依赖性的普通微扰时，该基底失效。这类微扰在现实宇宙学或引力场景中普遍存在，会破坏四维有效费米子质量矩阵的对角结构，使得未微扰的KK模不再构成物理本征态。因此，亟需一种能严格保持五维手征结构的处理方法，以确定真实的物理谱，并理解诸如原本不可见（“暗”）的KK态被激活等现象学后果。

Method: The authors expand the full 5D Dirac operator—including generic background perturbations—over the unperturbed KK eigenbasis derived from the static background. This expansion yields an off-diagonal 4D effective Dirac mass matrix $ \mathcal{M}_{mn} = \int dy\, \bar{\psi}_m(y) \left[ \slashed{\partial}_5 + \Phi(y) + \delta\Phi(y) \right] \psi_n(y) $, where $ \delta\Phi(y) $ encodes the spatially varying perturbation. To preserve the underlying 5D chirality and extract physical fermion states, they perform an exact Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) on this non-Hermitian but chiral-symmetric mass matrix. The SVD diagonalizes $ \mathcal{M} $ as $ \mathcal{M} = U \Sigma V^\dagger $, yielding left- and right-handed 4D mode functions and corrected mass eigenvalues encoded in $ \Sigma $. The analysis distinguishes perturbations by their parity under the extra-dimensional $ Z_2 $ symmetry.
\n\n
作者将包含一般背景微扰的完整五维Dirac算符在静态背景导出的未微扰KK本征基上展开。该展开产生一个非对角的四维有效Dirac质量矩阵 $ \mathcal{M}_{mn} = \int dy\, \bar{\psi}_m(y) \left[ \slashed{\partial}_5 + \Phi(y) + \delta\Phi(y) \right] \psi_n(y) $，其中 $ \delta\Phi(y) $ 编码了沿额外维度的空间变化微扰。为保持底层五维手征结构并提取物理费米子态，他们对该具有手征对称性但非厄米的质量矩阵进行精确的奇异值分解（SVD）。SVD将 $ \mathcal{M} $ 对角化为 $ \mathcal{M} = U \Sigma V^\dagger $，从而给出左右手四维模函数及由 $ \Sigma $ 编码的修正质量本征值。分析根据微扰在额外维度 $ Z_2 $ 对称性下的宇称性质进行分类。

Result: The key results are: (1) Generic perturbations induce off-diagonal couplings between distinct KK levels, invalidating the unperturbed basis as physical eigenstates. (2) An exact SVD yields corrected mass eigenvalues with small but highly structured shifts dictated by the perturbation’s spatial profile and parity. (3) Parity-odd perturbations ($ \delta\Phi(-y) = -\delta\Phi(y) $) only mix KK modes of the same parity, preserving the macroscopic $ Z_2 $ symmetry. (4) Parity-even perturbations ($ \delta\Phi(-y) = +\delta\Phi(y) $) mix opposite-parity KK modes, explicitly breaking $ Z_2 $, leading to asymmetric probability densities strongly localized toward the brane. (5) This spatial polarization converts nodes (zeros) in the original wavefunctions into finite amplitudes at the brane location, thereby rendering previously "dark" (brane-coupling-suppressed) KK modes observable.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 普通微扰在不同KK能级间诱导非对角耦合，使得未微扰基底不再是物理本征态；(2) 精确SVD给出修正后的质量本征值，其偏移虽小但具有由微扰空间分布和宇称决定的高度结构化特征；(3) 奇宇称微扰（$ \delta\Phi(-y) = -\delta\Phi(y) $）仅混合相同宇称的KK模，保持宏观 $ Z_2 $ 对称性；(4) 偶宇称微扰（$ \delta\Phi(-y) = +\delta\Phi(y) $）混合相反宇称的KK模，显式破坏 $ Z_2 $ 对称性，导致概率密度不对称且强烈局域于膜附近；(5) 这种空间极化将原波函数在膜位置的节点（零点）转变为有限振幅，从而使原本“暗”（即与膜耦合被压低）的KK模变得可观测。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that realistic perturbations in thick braneworlds fundamentally alter the fermionic KK spectrum through structured mode mixing, necessitating a non-perturbative treatment beyond standard KK reduction. The exact SVD approach preserves 5D chirality and reveals that the nature of the perturbation—particularly its parity—controls both the symmetry properties and phenomenological visibility of KK states. Crucially, even small even-parity perturbations can dramatically enhance brane-localized couplings of higher KK modes, potentially opening new detection channels for extra-dimensional physics in collider or astrophysical settings.
\n\n
本文表明，厚膜世界中的现实微扰通过结构化的模混合从根本上改变了费米子KK谱，这要求超越标准KK约化的非微扰处理方法。精确的SVD方法保持了五维手征性，并揭示出微扰的性质——尤其是其宇称——决定了KK态的对称性特征与现象学可观测性。关键的是，即使微小的偶宇称微扰也能显著增强高阶KK模与膜的耦合，可能为在对撞机或天体物理环境中探测额外维度物理开辟新的观测通道。

Abstract: We investigate fermionic Kaluza-Klein (KK) mode mixing in thick braneworld models subjected to generic background perturbations. Conventionally, isolated static backgrounds are completely described by a Schroedinger-like formulation, which yields an unperturbed orthogonal basis of KK eigenstates. However, generic perturbations possess a non-trivial spatial profile along the extra dimension. When the full interacting Dirac operator is expanded in this original basis, the spatial variation inevitably yields non-vanishing overlap integrals between distinct KK levels, thereby inducing off-diagonal couplings in the 4D effective mass matrix. Consequently, the original eigenstates are no longer exact physical eigenmodes of the perturbed system. To rigorously preserve the underlying 5D chiral structure and resolve the true physical states, we employ an exact Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) of the full, off-diagonal Dirac mass matrix. Our exact analysis reveals that this mode mixing introduces small but highly structured corrections to the mass eigenvalues. Specifically, parity-odd perturbation operators strictly induce same-parity mixing that preserves the macroscopic Z2 spatial symmetry, whereas parity-even operators trigger cross-parity mixing that shatters the Z2 symmetry, resulting in severe spatial polarization of the KK probability densities. Phenomenologically, such polarization shifts the wave functions toward the brane, turning probability zeros into non-zero values, which directly illuminates previously "dark" KK modes.

</details>


### [31] [Thermal Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05136)
*Clifford Cheung,Rachel A. Rosen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that Lorentz invariance and unitarity enforce strict positivity conditions on certain finite-temperature corrections to thermodynamic quantities—specifically, interaction-induced corrections to pressure scaling as $T^{2D-4+4k}$ ($k>0$) in $D$-dimensional relativistic massless bosonic theories are strictly positive. These constraints also apply to entropy density and specific heat, under the assumption of a weakly coupled UV completion and absence of long-range forces.
\n\n
本文证明了洛伦兹不变性和幺正性对有限温度热力学量施加了严格的正定性约束——具体而言，在$D$维相对论性无质量玻色子理论中，相互作用导致的压强修正项（标度为$T^{2D-4+4k}$，其中$k>0$）严格为正。这些约束在假设存在弱耦合紫外完备且无长程力的前提下，同样适用于熵密度和比热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The authors aim to uncover fundamental constraints on thermal physics imposed by basic principles of quantum field theory—namely Lorentz invariance and unitarity. While such principles tightly constrain scattering amplitudes at zero temperature via dispersion relations and positivity bounds, their implications for finite-temperature observables are less explored. This work bridges that gap by connecting thermal vacuum diagrams to forward scattering amplitudes, thereby extending amplitude-based positivity arguments to thermodynamics.
\n\n
作者旨在揭示量子场论基本原理（即洛伦兹不变性和幺正性）对热物理施加的根本性约束。尽管这些原理通过色散关系和正定性界对零温散射振幅有严格限制，但其对有限温度可观测量的影响尚不充分。本文通过将热真空图与前向散射振幅联系起来，将基于振幅的正定性论证推广至热力学领域，填补了这一空白。

Method: The core method involves mapping thermal vacuum diagrams (which compute the free energy) to forward scattering amplitudes using the imaginary-time formalism. By analytically continuing to real time and leveraging the optical theorem—which encodes unitarity—and Lorentz invariance, the authors derive dispersion relations for these amplitudes. The positivity of the absorptive part (due to unitarity) then implies sign constraints on the Taylor coefficients of the amplitudes, which correspond to thermal corrections scaling as $T^{2D-4+4k}$. The analysis assumes a weakly coupled UV completion so that higher-loop and higher-multiplicity contributions are suppressed.
\n\n
核心方法是利用虚时形式将热真空图（用于计算自由能）映射到前向散射振幅。通过对时间进行实解析延拓，并结合体现幺正性的光学定理及洛伦兹不变性，作者为这些振幅导出色散关系。吸收部分的正定性（源于幺正性）进而对振幅的泰勒展开系数施加符号约束，这些系数对应于标度为$T^{2D-4+4k}$的热修正项。分析假设存在弱耦合紫外完备，使得高圈图和高多重性贡献被压低。

Result: The paper establishes that in any weakly coupled relativistic effective field theory of massless bosons without long-range forces, the interaction corrections to the pressure at low temperature of the form $T^{2D-4+4k}$ (with integer $k > 0$) are strictly positive in $D$ spacetime dimensions. Consequently, analogous terms in the entropy density $s$ and specific heat $c_V$—obtained via thermodynamic derivatives—are also positive. This constitutes an infinite family of positivity bounds on thermal observables derived from first principles.
\n\n
本文确立了：在任意不含长程力、由弱耦合紫外理论导出的无质量玻色子相对论性有效场论中，低温下压强的相互作用修正项（形式为$T^{2D-4+4k}$，其中整数$k > 0$）在$D$维时空下严格为正。因此，通过热力学导数得到的熵密度$s$和比热$c_V$中的相应项也均为正。这构成了一族从第一性原理导出的热力学可观测量的无限正定性界。

Conclusion: Lorentz invariance and unitarity, when combined with the assumption of a weakly coupled UV completion, yield rigorous positivity constraints on finite-temperature thermodynamic functions in relativistic EFTs. These results extend the logic of the S-matrix bootstrap to thermal physics and suggest that consistency conditions from scattering amplitudes can meaningfully restrict the space of allowed thermal effective theories.
\n\n
洛伦兹不变性与幺正性，结合弱耦合紫外完备的假设，对相对论性有效场论中的有限温度热力学函数施加了严格的正定性约束。这些结果将S矩阵Bootstrap的逻辑推广至热物理领域，表明来自散射振幅的一致性条件可有效限制允许的热有效理论空间。

Abstract: We argue that Lorentz invariance and unitarity impose sharp constraints on thermodynamic quantities. By relating thermal vacuum diagrams to forward scattering amplitudes, we derive an infinite family of sign conditions on finite-temperature observables in perturbative theories of relativistic massless bosons. In particular, we prove that all low-temperature corrections from interactions to the pressure, or equivalently the negative free energy density, of the form T^{2D-4+4k} with k>0 in D spacetime dimensions, are strictly positive. These positivity conditions are inherited by analogous terms in the entropy density and specific heat. Our results apply to any effective field theory that is free of long-range forces and descends from a weakly coupled ultraviolet completion, in which case higher-loop and higher-multiplicity thermal diagrams are parametrically subleading.

</details>

### [32] [A Common Antimatter Response in AMS-02 Positrons and Antiprotons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04043)
*Yi Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The AMS-02 data reveal a surprising high-rigidity similarity in the spectral slopes of protons, antiprotons, and positrons above ~60 GV—unlike electrons—suggesting a universal antimatter propagation effect rather than individual exotic sources. The paper proposes a "no-extra-source" framework where antiparticles experience reduced post-production radiative or residence softening, leading to a required hardening $Δ_{\bar p} \simeq δ_{\rm eff}$ that compensates standard secondary softening. This predicts a joint spectral alignment testable across $p$, $\bar p$, $e^+$, and $e^-$.
\n\n
AMS-02数据显示，在约60 GV以上的高刚度区，质子、反质子和正电子的能谱斜率惊人地相似（而电子则不同），这暗示了一种普适的反物质传播效应，而非各自独立的奇异源。本文提出一种“无额外源”（no-extra-source）框架：反粒子保留其产生历史，但其产生后的辐射或驻留软化被减弱。对于反质子，所需硬化$Δ_{\bar p} \simeq δ_{\rm eff}$恰好补偿标准次级软化$δ_{\rm eff}$。该模型预言了一个可检验的联合能谱对齐关系，需同时拟合$p$、$\bar p$、$e^+$和$e^-$的高刚度斜率结构，而非仅依赖$\bar p/p$比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the puzzling AMS-02 observation that above ~60 GV, the rigidity spectra of protons ($p$), antiprotons ($\bar p$), and positrons ($e^+$) exhibit nearly identical slopes, while electrons ($e^-$) deviate significantly. Conventional cosmic-ray models treat $\bar p$ as purely secondary (from hadronic collisions) and often invoke exotic primary sources (e.g., dark matter) to explain $e^+$ excesses. However, the commonality across three species—two antimatter and one matter—challenges such interpretations and suggests a unified modification to propagation physics rather than particle-specific new sources.
\n\n
动机源于AMS-02观测到的一个令人困惑的现象：在约60 GV以上，质子（$p$）、反质子（$\bar p$）和正电子（$e^+$）的刚度谱展现出几乎相同的斜率，而电子（$e^-$）显著偏离。传统宇宙线模型将$\bar p$视为纯次级产物（来自强子碰撞），并常引入奇异主源（如暗物质）来解释$e^+$超出。然而，三种粒子（两种反物质与一种物质）之间的共性挑战了这类解释，暗示应存在一种统一的传播物理修正，而非针对特定粒子的新源。

Method: The paper introduces a “no-extra-source” principle: antiparticles retain their standard production mechanisms (e.g., $\bar p$ from $pp$ collisions, $e^+$ from cosmic-ray interactions), but their post-production energy losses or residence time in the Galaxy are reduced compared to standard retarded Green’s function responses. For $e^+$, this manifests as reduced radiative cooling; for $\bar p$, as reduced softening during Galactic confinement. In a power-law propagation framework, this is modeled as an effective hardening $Δ_{\bar p}$ that counteracts the conventional secondary spectral softening $δ_{\rm eff}$, yielding the condition $Δ_{\bar p} \simeq δ_{\rm eff}$. The key methodological shift is to analyze the joint high-rigidity spectral geometry of $p$, $\bar p$, $e^+$, and $e^-$ simultaneously.
\n\n
本文提出“无额外源”原理：反粒子保留其标准产生机制（例如$\bar p$来自$pp$碰撞，$e^+$来自宇宙线相互作用），但其产生后的能量损失或银河系内驻留时间相比标准推迟响应（retarded response）有所减少。对$e^+$表现为辐射冷却减弱；对$\bar p$表现为银晕禁闭期间的软化减弱。在幂律传播框架下，这被建模为一种有效硬化$Δ_{\bar p}$，用以抵消传统的次级谱软化$δ_{\rm eff}$，从而得到条件$Δ_{\bar p} \simeq δ_{\rm eff}$。方法论上的关键转变是同时分析$p$、$\bar p$、$e^+$和$e^-$在高刚度区的联合谱几何结构。

Result: The model successfully explains the observed near-identical rigidity dependence of $p$, $\bar p$, and $e^+$ above 60 GV without invoking new primary antimatter sources. 该模型在不引入新的原初反物质源的前提下，成功解释了60 GV以上$p$、$\bar p$和$e^+$近似相同的刚度依赖性。它预言反质子硬化$Δ_{\bar p}$必须近似等于有效次级软化$δ_{\rm eff}$（谱指数通常约为0.1–0.2）。关键的是，它改变了验证标准：有效模型必须重现四种粒子在高刚度区的斜率对齐，而不仅仅是$\bar p/p$比值——许多传统模型无法满足这一要求。

Conclusion: The high-rigidity spectral congruence among $p$, $\bar p$, and $e^+$ points to a universal modification in the propagation of antimatter—specifically, a reduction in post-production energy degradation—rather than particle-specific exotic sources. The “no-extra-source” framework provides a minimal, testable alternative that reinterprets the AMS-02 anomaly as a propagation effect. Future cosmic-ray analyses should prioritize multi-species spectral consistency over isolated ratio fits.

$p$、$\bar p$和$e^+$在高刚度区的谱一致性表明，反物质传播中存在一种普适修正——即产生后的能量退化减弱——而非针对特定粒子的奇异源。“无额外源”框架提供了一个极简且可检验的替代理论，将AMS-02异常重新解释为传播效应。未来的宇宙线分析应优先考虑多粒子谱的一致性，而非孤立地拟合单一比值。

Abstract: AMS-02 has reported a high-rigidity regularity that is difficult to interpret as a positron-only fact: above about $60\,\mathrm{GV}$, antiprotons, protons, and positrons have nearly identical rigidity dependence, whereas electrons do not. I argue that this pattern suggests a common antimatter response. The organizing principle is a no-extra-source one: antiparticles retain their production history, but their post-production retarded exposure can be reduced relative to an ordinary retarded response. For positrons this is reduced accumulated radiative exposure rather than an added positron-only source. For antiprotons, whose production is hadronic and secondary, the production kernel is kept fixed; the corresponding effect is a reduction of the second, post-production residence softening rather than a new antiproton source. In a power-law response language, the required hardening $Δ_{\bar p}$ must compensate the ordinary secondary softening $δ_{\rm eff}$, giving $Δ_{\bar p}\simeqδ_{\rm eff}$. The resulting test is direct: conventional explanations must reproduce the joint high-rigidity slope geometry of $p$, $\bar p$, $e^+$, and $e^-$, not only the one-dimensional $\bar p/p$ ratio.

TL;DR: This paper systematically quantifies how uncertainties in the local dark matter velocity distribution affect direct detection rates for dark matter–single phonon scattering. Motivation: Direct detection experiments targeting sub-GeV dark matter via single-phonon excitations are highly sensitive to the local dark matter velocity distribution. Conclusion: The study demonstrates that for sub-GeV dark matter direct detection via single-phonon processes, a comprehensive treatment of astrophysical uncertainties does not require exploring multiple complex halo models. Abstract: We present the first systematic study of how local dark matter velocity distribution uncertainties propagate into direct detection rates for dark matter--single phonon scattering. We consider three benchmark halo models -- Standard Halo Model, Tsallis and empirical -- and vary the astrophysical parameters within observationally motivated ranges. To compare halo models on equal footing, we introduce an rms-matching prescription that holds the mean dark matter kinetic energy fixed across models. With this prescription, differences between halo models prove subdominant to parameter variations within each model, so that astrophysical uncertainties can be effectively captured by varying parameters within the Standard Halo Model alone. We find $\mathcal{O}(1\%)$ to $\mathcal{O}(100\%)$ fractional deviations in the predicted rates across the dark matter mass range of interest. These results provide timely input for reliably interpreting upcoming phonon-based direct detection experiments targeting sub-GeV dark matter.

Method: The authors employ the Colour Glass Condensate (CGC) effective field theory to compute the cross section for diffractive $q\\bar{q}g$ production in the correlation limit, where two partons are hard and back-to-back in transverse momentum ($\\vec{p}_\\perp \\approx -\\vec{q}_\\perp$), while the third carries semi-hard momentum $k_\\perp \\sim Q_s$, the nuclear saturation scale. They analyze two distinct configurations: (i) hard $q\\bar{q}$ + semi-hard gluon, and (ii) hard $\\bar{q}g$ + semi-hard quark. By performing explicit CGC calculations and taking appropriate limits, they isolate universal diffractive TMDs and associated hard factors, examining their dependence on quark mass $m_q$.
\n\n
作者采用Colour Glass Condensate（CGC）有效场论，在关联极限下计算衍射$q\\bar{q}g$产生的截面：其中两个部分子是硬的且在横向动量上近似背对背（$\\vec{p}_\\perp \\approx -\\vec{q}_\\perp$），而第三个部分子携带半硬动量$k_\\perp \\sim Q_s$（即核饱和动量标度）。他们分析了两种不同构型：(i) 硬$q\\bar{q}$ + 半硬胶子；(ii) 硬$\\bar{q}g$ + 半硬夸克。通过显式的CGC计算并取适当极限，他们分离出普适的衍射TMD及其对应的“硬”因子，并考察其对夸克质量$m_q$的依赖性。

Result: For configuration (i), standard TMD factorization holds with a mass-dependent hard factor and the known gluon diffractive TMD. Conclusion: The study establishes TMD factorization for massive quark production in diffractive deep inelastic scattering within the CGC framework, revealing a novel mass-dependent quark diffractive TMD. Abstract: Using the Colour Glass Condensate effective theory, we study the diffractive production of a massive quark-antiquark pair accompanied by a gluon in coherent photon-nucleus collisions at high energy. This partonic configuration provides the leading twist contribution to the cross section in the correlation limit where two of the partons are hard and nearly back to back in the transverse plane, while the third one is semi-hard, with a transverse momentum of the order of the nuclear saturation momentum. We consider two scenarios: (i) a hard quark-antiquark pair together with a semi-hard gluon; in this case we demonstrate transverse momentum dependent (TMD) factorization with a mass-dependent ''hard'' factor and the standard expression for the gluon diffractive TMD, and (ii) a hard antiquark-gluon pair and a semi-hard quark; in this case we find TMD factorization with a mass-independent ''hard'' factor and a mass-dependent quark diffractive TMD, which represents a new result. We show that increasing the quark mass reduces (or even washes out) the effects of gluon saturation on the quark diffractive TMD. In particular, it leads to the suppression of the Cronin peak that we observe in the massless limit. Our results are the basis for future phenomenological studies of quarkonium and open charm production in the saturation regime in ultraperipheral collisions at the Large Hadron Collider, and in deep inelastic scattering at the Electron-Ion Collider.

Using these, they derive a closed-form expression for the topological susceptibility $χ_{\rm top}^{\rm F}$, which determines the axion mass via $m_a^2 f_a^2 = χ_{\rm top}^{\rm F}$. Abstract: We examine the QCD axion potential in Fried's nonperturbative QCD functional formalism. The axion is introduced in the standard way through (Θ=θ_{\rm QCD}+a/f_a). χ_{\rm top}^{\rm F} ======================
  \left[ A_{\rm F}^{-1} +
\sum_f (m_fΣ_{\rm F})^{-1} \right]^{-1}, \qquad m_a^2f_a^2=χ_{\rm top}^{\rm F}. ]
This expression has the expected heavy-quark, light-quark, and massless-quark limits. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Independent transport coefficients (e.g., thermal conductivity or electrical resistivity) offer limited insight into the microscopic correlations in strongly interacting media. Coupled-transport phenomena—where gradients in one thermodynamic quantity drive fluxes of another—reveal richer information about underlying dynamics. The motivation is to explore how such cross-coupled responses, namely the Soret effect ($\\nabla T \\rightarrow$ particle diffusion) and Dufour effect ($\\nabla \\mu \\rightarrow$ heat flow), behave near the chiral phase transition in dense QCD matter, which remains poorly understood.

独立的输运系数（如热导率或电导率）对强相互作用介质中的微观关联提供的信息有限。而耦合输运现象——即一种热力学量的梯度驱动另一种流——能揭示更丰富的底层动力学信息。本文旨在探索这类交叉耦合响应（即Soret效应（$\\nabla T \\rightarrow$ 粒子扩散）和Dufour效应（$\\nabla \\mu \\rightarrow$ 热流））在高密QCD物质中手征相变附近的特性，该区域目前仍缺乏系统理解。

Method: The authors employ the 2+1 flavor Nambu–Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model at finite temperature $T$ and quark chemical potential $\\mu_q$ to describe the quark medium. They compute transport coefficients by solving the relativistic Boltzmann transport equation under the relaxation time approximation (RTA). Crucially, the relaxation time is derived from temperature-dependent $2 \\leftrightarrow 2$ scattering cross sections obtained within the NJL framework. The Soret coefficient $S_T$ and Dufour coefficient $D_q$ are then extracted from linear-response relations between thermodynamic forces and fluxes, and presented in scaled dimensionless forms as functions of $T$ and $\\mu_q$ across the QCD phase diagram.

作者采用有限温度$T$和夸克化学势$\\mu_q$下的2+1味Nambu–Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型来描述夸克介质。他们通过在弛豫时间近似（RTA）下求解相对论性Boltzmann输运方程来计算输运系数。关键在于，弛豫时间由NJL模型框架内得到的温度依赖的$2 \\leftrightarrow 2$散射截面导出。随后，从热力学力与流之间的线性响应关系中提取Soret系数$S_T$和Dufour系数$D_q$，并以无量纲标度形式展示其在QCD相图中随$T$和$\\mu_q$的变化。

This constitutes the first systematic calculation of cross-coupled transport in this regime.

标度化的Soret系数和Dufour系数作为温度和夸克化学势的函数表现出非单调行为。最显著的是，两种系数在与手征对称性恢复相关的赝临界温度附近均显示出明显敏感性，表明耦合输运观测量可作为高密夸克物质中手征相变的探针。这是该区域内首次对交叉耦合输运进行的系统性计算。

Abstract: Coupled-transport phenomena reveal that heat, charge, and particle flows are intrinsically interconnected, providing deeper insight into the microscopic dynamics of a medium than independent transport processes. We study the behavior of the coupled-transport coefficients in hot and dense quark matter within the framework of the 2+1 flavor Nambu--Jona--Lasinio model at finite temperature and quark chemical potential. These coefficients characterize coupled-transport phenomena, where particle diffusion is driven by temperature gradients (Soret effect) and heat flow is induced by gradients in chemical potential (Dufour effect). These coefficients are estimated by solving the relativistic Boltzmann transport equation using the relaxation time approximation with temperature-dependent cross sections. We study the scaled Soret and Dufour coefficients as functions of temperature and quark chemical potential across the QCD phase diagram. We aim to understand the intricate behavior of the coupled-transport coefficients near the chiral symmetry restoration region. Our results indicate that coupled-transport coefficients are sensitive to the chiral phase transition and provide the first systematic insight into the cross-coupled-transport properties in dense quark matter.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to determine sharp kinematic boundaries in scattering or decay processes where some final-state particles escape detection—either due to detector inefficiencies or inherently weak interactions (e.g., neutrinos). Abstract: This paper proposes a general approach for calculating kinematic
