本文利用重介子有效场论（HMEFT）研究激发态粲奇异介子的双体赝标量发射衰变，其中通过在 $DP$ 与 $D^*P$ 振幅之间引入有效相对位移来参数化 $1/m_c$ 修正，称为有效自旋对称性破缺修正。利用 $D_{s2}^*(2573)$ 数据校准 $T(3/2^+)$ 双重态，得到参数 $h'=0.407\\pm0.034$ 和 $\\varepsilon_T=-0.207\\pm0.109$，表明存在约 $20\\%$ 的自然有效修正。Belle 和 LHCb 的部分波数据对 $D_{s1}(2460)$–$D_{s1}(2536)$ 系统的混合角给出约束 $0^\\circ<\\theta_P\\lesssim22.0^\\circ$ 和 $0^\\circ<\\theta_P\\lesssim14.6^\\circ$，确认 $D_{s1}(2536)$ 主要为 $T(3/2^+)$ 态，仅含少量 $S(1/2^+)$ 成分。在径向激发区，纯 $2S$ 赋值给出 $R_{2700}^{\\rm LO}=0.919$，与观测到的 $D_{s1}^*(2700)$ 的 $D^{*0}K^+/D^0K^+$ 比值一致，但仅预测 $\\Gamma_{\\rm ps}[D_{s0}(2590)]\\simeq20$ MeV。引入 $D_{s1}^*(2700)$ 与 $D_{s1}^*(2860)$ 的混合、相对强相位及有效自旋对称性破缺修正后，该宽度显著增大，同时保持与其他观测量的一致性。此情形下预测 $R_{1,2860}=0.911$，远偏离纯 $X$ 态的领头阶值 $R_{1,2860}^{\\rm pure\\,X}=0.242$，因此在 2.86 GeV 附近的 $D^*K/DK$ 比值可作为混合与非混合赋值的关键判据。总体而言，该方案缓解但未完全消除 $D_{s0}(2590)$ 宽度张力，暗示可能存在非赝标量道、阈值效应或耦合道动力学贡献。文中还给出了 $D_{s3}^*(2860)$、$D_{s1}(2933)$ 和 $D_{sJ}(3040)$ 的参考衰变模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from persistent tensions in the interpretation of excited charm-strange meson spectroscopy and decay patterns, particularly concerning the widths and branching ratios of states like $D_{s0}(2590)$ and $D_{s1}^*(2700)/D_{s1}^*(2860)$. Heavy Quark Symmetry (HQS) predicts degenerate doublets, but $1/m_c$ corrections break this symmetry. A systematic framework—Heavy Meson Effective Field Theory (HMEFT)—is needed to incorporate these corrections in a controlled way to reconcile theoretical predictions with experimental data from Belle, LHCb, and others. Specifically, the paper aims to quantify effective spin-symmetry-breaking effects and explore whether configuration mixing can resolve discrepancies in pseudoscalar decay widths.
\n\n
动机源于激发态粲奇异介子谱学及其衰变模式解释中存在的持续张力，特别是 $D_{s0}(2590)$ 和 $D_{s1}^*(2700)/D_{s1}^*(2860)$ 等态的宽度与分支比问题。重夸克对称性（HQS）预言双重态简并，但 $1/m_c$ 修正会破坏该对称性。因此需要一个系统性框架——重介子有效场论（HMEFT）——以可控方式纳入这些修正，从而协调理论预言与 Belle、LHCb 等实验数据。本文特别旨在量化有效自旋对称性破缺效应，并探究组态混合是否能解决赝标量衰变宽度中的不一致性。

Method: The authors employ Heavy Meson Effective Field Theory (HMEFT) extended to include phenomenological $1/m_c$ corrections. These are implemented as effective relative shifts—denoted $\\varepsilon_T$, $\\varepsilon_S$, etc.—between amplitudes for $DP$ and $D^*P$ final states, representing leading-order spin-symmetry-breaking effects beyond strict HQS. The $T(3/2^+)$ doublet is calibrated using measured decays of $D_{s2}^*(2573)$ to extract coupling $h'$ and shift $\\varepsilon_T$. Mixing between $S(1/2^+)$ and $T(3/2^+)$ states is parameterized by an angle $\\theta_P$. For radially excited states, they consider both pure $2S$ assignments and mixing between $D_{s1}^*(2700)$ ($2^1S_1$) and $D_{s1}^*(2860)$ ($1^3D_1$ or mixed), including a relative strong phase $\\delta$ and effective spin-breaking corrections. Decay widths and ratios such as $R_{2700} = \\mathcal{B}(D^{*0}K^+)/\\mathcal{B}(D^0K^+)$ and $R_{1,2860} = \\mathcal{B}(D^*K)/\\mathcal{B}(DK)$ are computed at leading order with these corrections and compared to data.
\n\n
作者采用扩展的重介子有效场论（HMEFT），引入唯象的 $1/m_c$ 修正。这些修正被实现为 $DP$ 与 $D^*P$ 末态振幅之间的有效相对位移（记为 $\\varepsilon_T$、$\\varepsilon_S$ 等），代表超出严格重夸克对称性（HQS）的领头阶自旋对称性破缺效应。利用 $D_{s2}^*(2573)$ 的测量衰变对 $T(3/2^+)$ 双重态进行校准，提取耦合常数 $h'$ 和位移参数 $\\varepsilon_T$。$S(1/2^+)$ 与 $T(3/2^+)$ 态之间的混合由混合角 $\\theta_P$ 参数化。对于径向激发态，考虑纯 $2S$ 赋值以及 $D_{s1}^*(2700)$（$2^1S_1$）与 $D_{s1}^*(2860)$（$1^3D_1$ 或混合态）之间的混合，包含相对强相位 $\\delta$ 和有效自旋破缺修正。计算包含这些修正的领头阶衰变宽度及比值（如 $R_{2700} = \\mathcal{B}(D^{*0}K^+)/\\mathcal{B}(D^0K^+)$ 和 $R_{1,2860} = \\mathcal{B}(D^*K)/\\mathcal{B}(DK)$），并与实验数据比较。

Result: Key results include: (i) extraction of $h' = 0.407 \\pm 0.034$ and $\\varepsilon_T = -0.207 \\pm 0.109$ from $D_{s2}^*(2573)$ data, implying a $\\sim20\\%$ effective spin-symmetry-breaking correction; (ii) constraints on the $S$–$T$ mixing angle $\\theta_P < 22.0^\\circ$ (Belle) and $<14.6^\\circ$ (LHCb), confirming $D_{s1}(2536)$ is predominantly $T(3/2^+)$; (iii) a pure-$2S$ assignment for $D_{s1}^*(2700)$ yields $R_{2700}^{\\rm LO} = 0.919$, consistent with data, but predicts only $\\Gamma_{\\rm ps}[D_{s0}(2590)] \\simeq 20$ MeV, far below some experimental indications; (iv) introducing mixing between $D_{s1}^*(2700)$ and $D_{s1}^*(2860)$, along with a strong phase and $\\varepsilon$ corrections, boosts $\\Gamma_{\\rm ps}[D_{s0}(2590)]$ significantly while maintaining agreement for vector-state widths and $R_{2700}$; (v) this mixed scenario predicts $R_{1,2860} = 0.911$, drastically different from the pure-$X$ value $0.242$, offering a clear experimental test; (vi) residual tension in $D_{s0}(2590)$ width suggests additional physics beyond pseudoscalar emission.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(i) 从 $D_{s2}^*(2573)$ 数据中提取出 $h' = 0.407 \\pm 0.034$ 和 $\\varepsilon_T = -0.207 \\pm 0.109$，表明存在约 $20\\%$ 的有效自旋对称性破缺修正；(ii) 对 $S$–$T$ 混合角的约束为 $\\theta_P < 22.0^\\circ$（Belle）和 $<14.6^\\circ$（LHCb），确认 $D_{s1}(2536)$ 主要为 $T(3/2^+)$ 态；(iii) $D_{s1}^*(2700)$ 的纯 $2S$ 赋值给出 $R_{2700}^{\\rm LO} = 0.919$，与数据一致，但仅预测 $\\Gamma_{\\rm ps}[D_{s0}(2590)] \\simeq 20$ MeV，远低于某些实验迹象；(iv) 引入 $D_{s1}^*(2700)$ 与 $D_{s1}^*(2860)$ 的混合、强相位及 $\\varepsilon$ 修正后，$\\Gamma_{\\rm ps}[D_{s0}(2590)]$ 显著增大，同时保持矢量态宽度和 $R_{2700}$ 的一致性；(v) 此混合情形预测 $R_{1,2860} = 0.911$，与纯 $X$ 态值 $0.242$ 差异巨大，提供了清晰的实验检验；(vi) $D_{s0}(2590)$ 宽度仍存残余张力，暗示存在超出赝标量发射的额外物理机制。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that incorporating effective spin-symmetry-breaking corrections within HMEFT provides a consistent description of most excited charm-strange meson decays. While pure $S$-wave assignments fail to explain the $D_{s0}(2590)$ width, mixing between nearby $1^-$ states ($D_{s1}^*(2700)$ and $D_{s1}^*(2860)$) alleviates—but does not fully resolve—the tension. The predicted enhancement in the $D^*K/DK$ ratio near 2.86 GeV serves as a decisive test for configuration mixing. Remaining discrepancies point toward the need for additional mechanisms such as non-pseudoscalar decay channels (e.g., $D^*K^*$), threshold effects, or coupled-channel dynamics beyond the current HMEFT framework.
\n\n
研究表明，在重介子有效场论（HMEFT）中引入有效自旋对称性破缺修正，能够一致地描述大多数激发态粲奇异介子的衰变。虽然纯 $S$ 波赋值无法解释 $D_{s0}(2590)$ 的宽度，但邻近 $1^-$ 态（$D_{s1}^*(2700)$ 与 $D_{s1}^*(2860)$）之间的混合可缓解——但未完全消除——这一张力。在 2.86 GeV 附近预测的 $D^*K/DK$ 比值增强，为组态混合提供了决定性检验。剩余的不一致性表明需要引入额外机制，例如非赝标量衰变道（如 $D^*K^*$）、阈值效应或当前 HMEFT 框架之外的耦合道动力学。

Abstract: We study two-body pseudoscalar-emission decays of excited charm-strange mesons in heavy meson effective field theory, where phenomenological \(1/m_c\) corrections are encoded as effective relative shifts between \(DP\) and \(D^*P\) amplitudes, referred to here as effective spin-symmetry-breaking corrections. Using \(D_{s2}^*(2573)\) data to calibrate the \(T(3/2^+)\) doublet, we obtain \(h'=0.407\pm0.034\) and \(ε_T=-0.207\pm0.109\), indicating a natural effective correction of order \(20\%\). Applying this input to the \(D_{s1}(2460)\) and \(D_{s1}(2536)\) system, the Belle and LHCb partial-wave data constrain the mixing angle to \(0^\circ<θ_P\lesssim22.0^\circ\) and \(0^\circ<θ_P\lesssim14.6^\circ\), respectively, confirming that \(D_{s1}(2536)\) is dominantly a \(T(3/2^+)\) state with only a small \(S(1/2^+)\) admixture. In the radial sector, the pure-\(2S\) assignment gives \(R_{2700}^{\rm LO}=0.919\), consistent with the observed \(D^{*0}K^+/D^0K^+\) ratio of \(D_{s1}^*(2700)\), but predicts only \(Γ_{\rm ps}[D_{s0}(2590)]\simeq20\) MeV. Allowing mixing between \(D_{s1}^*(2700)\) and \(D_{s1}^*(2860)\), together with a relative strong phase and effective spin-symmetry-breaking corrections, substantially increases this width while preserving agreement with the vector-state widths and \(R_{2700}\). This scenario further gives \(R_{1,2860}=0.911\), far from the pure-\(X\) leading-order value \(R_{1,2860}^{\rm pure\,X}=0.242\), so the spin-one \(D^*K/DK\) ratio near \(2.86\) GeV offers a clear discriminator between the mixed and unmixed assignments. Overall, this scenario reduces but does not remove the \(D_{s0}(2590)\) width tension, leaving room for non-pseudoscalar channels, threshold effects, or coupled-channel dynamics. Reference decay patterns for \(D_{s3}^*(2860)\), \(D_{s1}(2933)\), and \(D_{sJ}(3040)\) are also given.

</details>


### [47] [Angular and invariant-mass observables in the four-body Higgs decay $h\to\ell\barν_\ell\bar{\ell}^\primeν_{\ell^\prime}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05061)

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper proposes a novel kinematic reorganization for the Higgs decay $h\to\ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$ ($\ell\neq\ell^\prime$) by grouping final states into a charged-lepton pair ($\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime$) and a neutrino pair ($\bar\nu_\ell\nu_{\ell^\prime}$), enabling an experimentally accessible angular analysis despite two undetected neutrinos. Using effective field theory (EFT), the authors derive a differential decay rate expressed in measurable variables—including the neutrino-pair invariant mass squared—and integrate over unobservable neutrino angles, yielding a clean probe of the $hWW$ coupling and potential BSM physics.
\n\n
本文提出了一种新的运动学重组方法，用于分析希格斯玻色子衰变过程 $h\to\ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$（其中 $\ell\neq\ell^\prime$）。通过将末态粒子重新划分为带电轻子对（$\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime$）和中微子对（$\bar\nu_\ell\nu_{\ell^\prime}$），即使存在两个不可探测的中微子，也能实现实验上可测量的角度分析。作者利用有效场论（Effective Field Theory, EFT）框架推导出以可观测量（包括中微子对不变质量平方）表达的微分衰变率，并对不可观测的中微子相关角度进行积分，从而提供了一种干净且可测量的角度分布，可用于精确检验 $hWW$ 耦合及可能的超出标准模型（BSM）的新物理效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard angular analyses of the four-body Higgs decay $h\to\ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$ are hindered by the presence of two invisible neutrinos, making full reconstruction of the kinematics impossible at collider experiments. This limits sensitivity to the structure of the $hWW$ vertex and possible anomalous couplings from Beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) physics. The motivation is to devise an alternative parametrization that retains sensitivity to these couplings while relying only on experimentally accessible observables.
\n\n
标准模型中四体希格斯衰变 $h\to\ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$ 的常规角度分析因存在两个不可见中微子而受阻，导致在对撞机实验中无法完全重建运动学信息。这限制了对 $hWW$ 顶点结构以及可能来自超出标准模型（Beyond-the-Standard-Model, BSM）物理的反常耦合的探测灵敏度。因此，本文旨在构建一种替代参数化方案，在仅依赖实验可观测量的前提下，仍保留对这些耦合结构的敏感性。

Method: The authors reorganize the final-state kinematics by treating the system as composed of two subsystems: the charged-lepton pair $\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime$ and the neutrino pair $\bar\nu_\ell\nu_{\ell^\prime}$. They express the differential decay rate using Lorentz-invariant variables, notably the invariant mass squared of the neutrino pair $q^2 = (p_{\nu_\ell} + p_{\nu_{\ell^\prime}})^2$, which—though not directly measurable—is constrained by momentum conservation and can be inferred from the visible lepton momenta. Within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) framework, they compute the amplitude for the $h \to W^+W^- \to \ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$ process, retain leading dimension-six operators affecting the $hWW$ coupling, and integrate over the unobservable angles associated with the neutrino pair in their rest frame. This yields a reduced angular distribution dependent only on measurable lepton kinematics.
\n\n
作者将末态运动学重新组织为两个子系统：带电轻子对 $\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime$ 和中微子对 $\bar\nu_\ell\nu_{\ell^\prime}$。他们使用洛伦兹不变变量（特别是中微子对的不变质量平方 $q^2 = (p_{\nu_\ell} + p_{\nu_{\ell^\prime}})^2$）来表达微分衰变率。尽管 $q^2$ 无法直接测量，但可通过动量守恒由可见轻子动量间接约束。在标准模型有效场论（Standard Model Effective Field Theory, SMEFT）框架下，作者计算了过程 $h \to W^+W^- \to \ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$ 的振幅，保留影响 $hWW$ 耦合的领头维数六算符，并在中微子对静止系中对不可观测的角度进行积分，最终得到一个仅依赖于可测轻子运动学的简化角度分布。

Result: The paper derives an analytic expression for the partially integrated differential decay rate $d\Gamma / (dq^2 d\cos\theta_\ell d\phi_\ell)$, where $\theta_\ell$ and $\phi_\ell$ are polar and azimuthal angles of one charged lepton in the dilepton rest frame. The resulting angular distribution cleanly separates contributions from different helicity amplitudes of the virtual $W$ bosons and encodes sensitivity to both CP-even and CP-odd components of the $hWW$ coupling. The dependence on the neutrino-pair invariant mass $q^2$ allows discrimination between Standard Model predictions and BSM scenarios parameterized by SMEFT Wilson coefficients.
\n\n
本文推导出了部分积分后的微分衰变率解析表达式 $d\Gamma / (dq^2 d\cos\theta_\ell d\phi_\ell)$，其中 $\theta_\ell$ 和 $\phi_\ell$ 是某一带电轻子在双轻子静止系中的极角和方位角。所得角度分布能清晰分离虚 $W$ 玻色子不同螺旋度振幅的贡献，并对 $hWW$ 耦合中的 CP 偶和 CP 奇分量均具有灵敏度。该分布对中微子对不变质量 $q^2$ 的依赖性可用于区分标准模型预言与由 SMEFT 威尔逊系数参数化的超出标准模型（BSM）新物理情景。

Conclusion: By reformulating the kinematics of the four-body Higgs decay into observable subsystems and integrating out unmeasurable degrees of freedom, the paper provides a practical and theoretically robust method to perform angular analyses in channels with multiple neutrinos. This approach enhances the LHC’s capability to probe the tensor structure of the $hWW$ vertex and constrain BSM physics through precision measurements of angular observables in $h\to\ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$ decays.
\n\n
通过将四体希格斯衰变的运动学重新表述为可观测的子系统，并对不可测自由度进行积分，本文提出了一种实用且理论自洽的方法，可在含多个中微子的衰变道中执行角度分析。该方法提升了大型强子对撞机（LHC）通过精确测量 $h\to\ell\bar\nu_\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime\nu_{\ell^\prime}$ 衰变中的角度观测量，来探测 $hWW$ 顶点张量结构并限制超出标准模型（BSM）物理的能力。

Abstract: We study the angular distribution of the Higgs boson decay $h\to\ell\barν_\ell\bar{\ell}^\primeν_{\ell^\prime}$ with $\ell\neq\ell^\prime$. Due to the presence of two undetected neutrinos, a complete angular analysis is not feasible at experiments. To overcome this, we reorganize the kinematics from the conventional lepton-neutrino pairs into a charged-lepton pair and a neutrino pair, i.e.~$\ell\bar{\ell}^\prime$ and $\barν_\ellν_{\ell^\prime}$. This allows us to express the differential decay rate in terms of experimentally accessible variables, including the invariant mass squared of the neutrino pair. Using the effective field theory framework, we derive this rate and integrate over the neutrino-associated angles. This parametrization provides a clean and measurable angular distribution, offering a new probe of the $hWW$ coupling and possible beyond-the-Standard-Model contributions.

### [48] [The Cosmological Hart-Tipler Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04044)
*David Kipping*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper presents a minimal cosmological model of self-replicating artificial "infections" (e.g., von Neumann probes) with only three parameters—spawn rate, propagation speed $u$, and start time—and shows that even modest assumptions lead to near-complete infection of the observable Universe, thereby sharpening the Hart-Tipler argument against the existence of aggressive extraterrestrial technological civilizations on cosmological scales.
\n\n
本文提出了一个极简的宇宙学模型，用于描述自复制人工“感染”（如冯·诺依曼探测器），仅包含三个参数：自发产生率、传播速度 $u$ 和起始时间。研究表明，即使采用较保守的假设，也会导致可观测宇宙近乎完全被“感染”，从而在宇宙学尺度上强化了 Hart-Tipler 论点，即攻击性地外技术文明应极为罕见甚至不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the Hart-Tipler conjecture, which argues that the absence of observed von Neumann machines in the Milky Way implies no other technological civilizations have arisen. Recent extensions of this idea to cosmological scales rely on complex behavioral assumptions; the authors aim to return to a simpler, more interpretable framework that captures the core logic of the original Fermi-Hart-Tipler paradox without unnecessary complexity.
\n\n
动机源于 Hart-Tipler 猜想，该猜想认为银河系中未观测到冯·诺依曼机器这一事实暗示其他技术文明未曾出现。近期将此思想扩展至宇宙学尺度的研究依赖于复杂的行为假设；作者旨在回归一个更简单、更具可解释性的框架，在不引入不必要复杂性的情况下捕捉原始 Fermi-Hart-Tipler 悖论的核心逻辑。

Method: The authors construct a bare-bones analytical model of cosmological infection by self-replicating probes, incorporating cosmic expansion via the Friedmann–Lemaître–Robertson–Walker (FLRW) metric. The model uses only three free parameters: a homogeneous spontaneous spawn rate per unit comoving volume, a constant proper propagation speed $u$ (as a fraction of $c$), and an initial time $t_0$ after the Big Bang. They compute the infected comoving volume fraction as a function of redshift using light-cone-like causal propagation in an expanding universe.
\n\n
作者构建了一个极简的自复制探测器宇宙学“感染”解析模型，通过 Friedmann–Lemaître–Robertson–Walker (FLRW) 度规纳入宇宙膨胀效应。该模型仅包含三个自由参数：单位共动体积内的均匀自发产生率、恒定的本征传播速度 $u$（以光速 $c$ 的分数表示）以及大爆炸之后的初始时间 $t_0$。他们利用膨胀宇宙中类光锥的因果传播，计算作为红移函数的被感染共动体积占比。

Result: For propagation speeds of $u = 0.1c$ starting at $t_0 = 4.5\\,\\text{Gyr}$, the model shows that if the spawn rate exceeds ~1 per million galaxies, over half the Universe is infected by today. For near-light-speed propagation ($u \\approx c$), the threshold rises to ~1 per billion galaxies. Remarkably, with $u = 0.1c$ and a spawn rate of just 1 per 100,000 galaxies, over 99.9% of cosmological volume becomes infected. This yields a sharp constraint: the non-observation of such infections implies either extremely low spawn rates or that such aggressive self-replication does not occur.
\n\n
对于 $u = 0.1c$、起始于 $t_0 = 4.5\\,\\text{Gyr}$ 的传播情形，模型表明：若产生率超过约每百万个星系一次，则当今宇宙一半以上已被感染。对于近光速传播（$u \\approx c$），阈值上升至约每十亿个星系一次。值得注意的是，当 $u = 0.1c$ 且产生率仅为每 10 万个星系一次时，超过 99.9% 的宇宙学体积都会被感染。这给出了一个尖锐的约束：此类感染未被观测到的事实意味着其产生率极低，或此类攻击性自复制行为根本不存在。

Conclusion: The "cosmological Hart-Tipler problem" provides a powerful minimal-model constraint on the prevalence of aggressive, self-propagating artificial systems in the Universe. The extreme efficiency of even slow interstellar probes at colonizing cosmological volumes implies that their absence today strongly limits how often such systems can emerge. The authors also discuss anthropic implications, suggesting that if such infections are possible, our existence may require fine-tuning to avoid being overwritten—highlighting a potential new facet of the Fermi paradox.
\n\n
“宇宙学 Hart-Tipler 问题”为宇宙中攻击性、自传播人工系统的普遍性提供了一个强有力的极简模型约束。即使传播速度较慢的星际探测器也能极其高效地“殖民”宇宙学体积，这意味着它们在今天的缺失强烈限制了此类系统能够出现的频率。作者还探讨了人择原理的含义：如果此类感染是可能的，那么我们的存在可能需要精细调节以避免被覆盖——这揭示了费米悖论的一个潜在新维度。

Abstract: Self-reproducing automata, so-called von Neumann machines, have been repeatedly estimated to be capable of traversing the Galaxy many times given its age. Our mere existence thus seems to exclude an aggressive variant of such a probe having ever been launched in the Milky Way. The Hart-Tipler conjecture considers this to represent contra-positive evidence to the hypothesis that other extra-terrestrial technological entities have emerged in our galaxy. Recently, several authors have extended interstellar colonization calculations to cosmological volumes, but these models are loaded with specific assumptions about behavior and emergence times. Here, we present a bare-bones model of generic artificial infections (such as but not limited to von Neumann probes) at cosmological scale in order to maximize interpretability, an approach closer to the original spirit of the Hart-Tipler calculations. Our model has just three parameters, a spontaneous spawn rate, a propagation speed (u) and a start time for the calculation. Accounting for cosmological expansion, we find that half the Universe is infected by today for u=0.1c propagation starting 4.5 Gyr after the Big Bang if the spawn rate exceeds approximately once per million galaxies. For near-c propagation, this becomes a billion galaxies. Over 99.9% of cosmological volumes are filled with 0.1c if even 1-in-100,000 galaxies have ever spawned an infection. The "cosmological Hart-Tipler" problem therefore offers a remarkably sharp minimal-model constraint on the prevalence of aggressive, self-propagating technological behavior. We explore its implications, such as how anthropic reasoning implies such infections occur and its fine-tuning nature.

Abstract: Accurate covariance matrix estimation is crucial to cosmological analyses, enabling unbiased parameter inference with well-calibrated uncertainties. Obtaining a reliable estimate generally requires far more independent samples than the dimension of the data vector, which is not always feasible. This challenge is especially relevant for the 3D Ly$α$ forest analysis, which measures the Ly$α$ auto-correlation and its cross-correlation with quasars in bins of comoving separation to jointly constrain cosmological parameters. The consequence is a very large data vector, and the data-driven covariance measured from sub-samples is non-invertible. The current approach applies a smoothing procedure to the off-diagonals of the correlation matrix to establish invertibility, but this does not fully capture the true correlation structure. In this work, I present a novel multi-eigenbasis denoising method for the data-driven covariance matrix, developed in the context of the 3D Ly$α$ forest analysis and conditioned on DESI DR1 mock simulations. The measured noisy covariance is first projected onto the eigenbasis of a mock-based reference, yielding an initial denoised estimate. A weighted residual correction is then constructed by projecting the noisy residual onto a second eigenbasis derived from a mock-trained classifier, capturing correlation structure not recovered in the initial reconstruction. I validate the method on mock covariances withheld from classifier training and find significant improvements over the current smoothing-based approach in matrix-level reconstruction metrics and in the recovery of cosmological parameter posteriors when compared to those obtained from the true covariance measured from many mock realizations.

The posterior distribution over initial conditions is sampled via HMC, yielding probabilistic reconstructions. Streamlines of the velocity field are integrated backward in time to locate sinks (potential minima), defining basins of attraction. 方法采用ΛCDM宇宙学模型中的Hamiltonian Monte Carlo（HMC）前向重构技术。从初始条件出发，算法生成密度扰动场$\\delta(\\mathbf{x})$与速度场$\\mathbf{v}(\\mathbf{x})$的实现，并向前演化后与Cosmicflows-4星系距离和径向速度数据进行比对。通过HMC采样初始条件的后验分布，得到概率性重构结果。对速度场流线进行时间反向积分以定位势阱（引力势极小值点），从而定义吸引域。V-web结构则通过速度剪切张量$\\Sigma_{ij} = \\frac{1}{2}(\\partial_i v_j + \\partial_j v_i)$计算，用于识别与Sloan Great Wall对齐的纤维结构。

  <summary>Details</summary>

However, prior studies often assume an unphysical instantaneous switch between evaporation regimes. A more realistic, continuous crossover must be examined to assess its impact on BBN constraints.
\n\n
标准霍金蒸发排除了轻质量原初黑洞（$M_i \lesssim 10^{15}\,\mathrm{g}$）作为重要暗物质成分的可能性，因其在大爆炸核合成（BBN）期间会产生过多相对论性遗迹。记忆负担效应——一种提议的量子引力修正——可抑制晚期辐射，或可挽救此类PBH。然而，以往研究常假设蒸发机制之间存在非物理的瞬时切换。必须考察更现实的连续过渡，以评估其对BBN限制的影响。

Method: The authors model PBH evaporation with a smoothed tanh interpolation between the standard semi-classical Hawking rate $\\dot{M}_{\\text{SC}}$ and the memory-burden suppressed rate $\\dot{M}_{\\text{MB}}$, parameterized by $(q, \\delta)$ controlling the midpoint and sharpness of the transition. Two combination rules are tested: additive ($\\dot{M} = \\dot{M}_{\\text{SC}} + \\dot{M}_{\\text{MB}}$) and multiplicative ($\\dot{M} = \\dot{M}_{\\text{SC}} \\cdot \\dot{M}_{\\text{MB}} / \\dot{M}_0$). Monochromatic PBH populations are mapped to an effective decaying scalar field and evolved through BBN using Modified AlterBBN to compute light-element abundances and derive constraints on the initial PBH fraction $f_{\\mathrm{PBH},0}$.

作者使用平滑的tanh插值函数在标准半经典霍金蒸发率$\\dot{M}_{\\text{SC}}$与记忆负担抑制率$\\dot{M}_{\\text{MB}}$之间建模PBH蒸发，由参数$(q, \\delta)$控制过渡的中点和陡峭程度。测试了两种组合规则：加性（$\\dot{M} = \\dot{M}_{\\text{SC}} + \\dot{M}_{\\text{MB}}$）与乘性（$\\dot{M} = \\dot{M}_{\\text{SC}} \\cdot \\dot{M}_{\\text{MB}} / \\dot{M}_0$）。单色PBH种群被映射为一个有效衰变标量场，并利用Modified AlterBBN程序演化通过BBN阶段，计算轻元素丰度并导出对初始PBH分数$f_{\\mathrm{PBH},0}$的限制。

对于初始质量在$10^{5}\,\mathrm{g}\lesssim M_i\lesssim 10^{10}\,\mathrm{g}$范围内的PBH，加性过渡允许$f_{\\mathrm{PBH},0}\\sim 10^{-1}$，而乘性情形则限制$f_{\\mathrm{PBH},0}\\lesssim 10^{-2}$。两种平滑处方均比常用的瞬时过渡近似给出更强的BBN限制。因此，在记忆负担模型下，速率组合规则的选择对轻PBH能否构成不可忽略的暗物质份额具有决定性影响。

Abstract: Light primordial black holes (PBHs) are disfavored as dark matter if they evaporate through standard Hawking radiation alone.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The ionizing photon escape fraction $f_{\rm esc}$ is a critical but poorly constrained quantity governing the timeline and morphology of cosmic reionization. Method: The authors adopt a three-parameter power-law model: $f_{\rm esc} = f_0\,(M/10^{10}M_\odot)^{α_M}\,[(1{+}z)/10]^{α_z}$. They use Schechter fits to HST and JWST UV luminosity functions at $z=5$–12, abundance matching to connect UV magnitude $M_{\rm UV}$ to halo mass $M_{\rm h}$, and a validated ordinary differential equation (ODE) solver for reionization history. Constraints are derived via a dense grid scan and ensemble Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), conditioned on: (1) UV LFs, (2) Planck Thomson optical depth $τ_e$, (3) seven $x_{\rm HI}$ measurements, and (4) one high-$z$ prior. Profile likelihoods and full marginal posteriors are both reported.

作者采用三参数幂律模型：$f_{\rm esc} = f_0\,(M/10^{10}M_\odot)^{α_M}\,[(1{+}z)/10]^{α_z}$。他们利用 HST 和 JWST 在 $z=5$–12 的紫外光度函数的 Schechter 拟合结果，通过丰度匹配（abundance matching）将紫外星等 $M_{\rm UV}$ 关联到晕质量 $M_{\rm h}$，并使用经 Planck 数据验证的常微分方程（ODE）求解器计算再电离历史。约束条件包括：(1) 紫外光度函数，(2) Planck 的 Thomson 光学深度 $τ_e$，(3) 七个中性氢分数 $x_{\rm HI}$ 测量值，以及 (4) 一个高红移先验。通过密集网格扫描和集成马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（ensemble MCMC）方法进行推断，并同时报告了轮廓似然（profile likelihood）和完整边缘后验。

Comparison with THESAN simulations shows that median per-halo $f_{\rm esc}$ evolves steeply, but luminosity-weighted averages flatten due to massive halos dominating at $z \lesssim 7$. Robustness checks confirm $α_z > 1.0$ at >95% confidence, and the best-fit model predicts $τ_e = 0.047$, consistent with Planck at $0.7σ$.
\n\n
轮廓似然给出紧致约束：$f_0=0.061_{-0.023}^{+0.018}$，$α_M=0.18_{-0.30}^{+0.22}$，$α_z=1.98_{-0.42}^{+0.48}$。然而，完整边缘后验显示出显著的参数简并，导致不确定性大幅展宽（例如 $α_z = 1.93_{-2.00}^{+2.09}$）。总体平均逃逸分数 $\langle f_{\rm esc} \rangle(z)$ 从 $z=5$ 时的约 2% 上升至 $z=12$ 时的约 9%，且在 $z \geq 10$ 时，亚阈值晕（$M_{\rm h} < 10^{10} M_\odot$）贡献了超过 80% 的电离光子。与 THESAN 模拟对比表明，单个晕的中位数 $f_{\rm esc}$ 呈现陡峭演化，但光度加权平均因 $z \lesssim 7$ 时大质量晕主导而趋于平坦。稳健性检验确认 $α_z > 1.0$ 的置信度超过 95%，最佳拟合模型预测 $τ_e = 0.047$，与 Planck 结果在 $0.7σ$ 内一致。

Conclusion: This work provides the first observationally driven, redshift- and mass-dependent model of $f_{\rm esc}(M_{\rm h}, z)$ across the reionization epoch. Despite strong parameter degeneracies, the data robustly favor a steep increase in escape fraction with redshift ($α_z > 1$), implying that faint, low-mass galaxies were the dominant sources of ionizing photons at high-$z$. The derived posterior distributions are made publicly available as empirical inputs for future reionization simulations, improving their physical fidelity.
\n\n
本工作提供了首个基于观测、覆盖整个再电离时期的红移和质量依赖的 $f_{\rm esc}(M_{\rm h}, z)$ 模型。尽管存在强参数简并，数据仍稳健地支持逃逸分数随红移陡增（$α_z > 1$），表明暗弱的低质量星系是高红移时电离光子的主要来源。所得到的后验分布已公开发布，可作为未来再电离模拟的经验输入，提升其物理真实性。

Abstract: The ionizing photon escape fraction $f_{\rm esc}$ governs cosmic reionization yet remains observationally unconstrained as a function of halo mass. We present the first empirical constraints on $f_{\rm esc}(M_{\rm h},z)$ across the epoch of reionization, using a three-parameter power-law model $f_{\rm esc} = f_0\,(M/10^{10}M_\odot)^{α_M}\,[(1{+}z)/10]^{α_z}$, conditioned on HST and JWST UV luminosity functions at $z=5$--12, the Planck Thomson optical depth, seven neutral-fraction measurements, and one high-redshift prior. Using Schechter fits to the latest HST and JWST UV luminosity functions, abundance matching to link $M_{\rm UV}$ to halo mass, and a reionization ODE solver validated against Planck, we constrain the model via a dense grid scan and ensemble MCMC. The profile likelihood yields tight constraints: $f_0=0.061_{-0.023}^{+0.018}$, $α_M=0.18_{-0.30}^{+0.22}$, $α_z=1.98_{-0.42}^{+0.48}$. In contrast, the full marginal posterior is substantially broadened by a strong $f_0$--$α_M$--$α_z$ degeneracy ($α_z = 1.93_{-2.00}^{+2.09}$, $α_M = -0.52_{-0.69}^{+0.69}$). The population-averaged $\langle f_{\rm esc} \rangle(z)$ rises from $\sim$2\% at $z=5$ to $\sim$9\% at $z=12$, with sub-threshold halos contributing $>80\%$ of the ionizing budget at $z\geq10$. Comparing with THESAN, we find that the per-halo median $f_{\rm esc}$ shows steep evolution consistent with our profile result, while luminosity-weighted averaging systematically flattens the trend because massive halos dominate the ionizing budget at $z\lesssim7$. Robustness checks confirm $α_z>1.0$ at $>95\%$ confidence; the steep-evolution model predicts $τ_e=0.047$, consistent with Planck at $0.7σ$. We provide tabulated $f_{\rm esc}(M_{\rm h},z)$ posteriors as empirical inputs for reionization simulations.

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper introduces a new theoretical framework to model cosmic variance by extending the galaxy biasing formalism within Eulerian perturbation theory, enabling a clearer separation between cosmic variance and galaxy bias effects in BAO measurements. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: With upcoming high-precision redshift surveys like DESI and Euclid, controlling systematic errors—particularly cosmic variance due to finite survey volumes—is critical for unbiased cosmological inference. Standard treatments often conflate cosmic variance with galaxy bias, limiting the accuracy of BAO-based parameter estimation. The authors aim to disentangle these effects through a refined theoretical description.

随着DESI和Euclid等高精度红移巡天的到来，控制系统误差（尤其是由有限巡天体积引起的cosmic variance）对于无偏宇宙学推断至关重要。标准处理方法常常将cosmic variance与galaxy bias混为一谈，限制了基于BAO的参数估计精度。作者旨在通过一种改进的理论描述来解耦这些效应。

Method: The authors reformulate cosmic variance by introducing a mapping between the density field of a single cosmic realization and the ensemble-averaged field using halo/galaxy Eulerian perturbation theory. This yields a new set of bias parameters that encode how a finite-volume realization deviates from the statistical mean. They apply this formalism to model the non-linear shift of the Baryon Acoustic Oscillation (BAO) peak, separating contributions from cosmic variance and conventional galaxy bias. The predictions are tested against cosmological N-body simulations with suppressed cosmic variance.
\n\n
作者利用halo/galaxy Eulerian微扰理论，在单次宇宙实现的密度场与系综平均场之间建立映射，从而重构cosmic variance。这产生了一组新的bias参数，用于刻画有限体积实现如何偏离统计均值。他们将此形式应用于建模Baryon Acoustic Oscillation (BAO)峰的非线性偏移，分离出cosmic variance与传统galaxy bias的贡献，并在cosmic variance被抑制的N-body模拟中检验其预测。

Result: The analytical framework successfully isolates the cosmic variance contribution to BAO scale uncertainties. When compared to variance-suppressed $N$-body simulations, the model predicts—and observations confirm—a reduction in the error on the BAO peak position, validating the efficacy of the new bias parameters and formalism.
\n\n
该分析框架成功地隔离了cosmic variance对BAO尺度不确定性的贡献。与variance-suppressed $N$-body模拟对比时，模型预测（并经观测证实）BAO峰位置的误差减小，验证了新bias参数及形式体系的有效性。

Conclusion: Perturbative analyses that estimate galaxy bias parameters must account for cosmic variance more carefully. The proposed formalism provides a principled way to separate finite-volume effects from intrinsic bias, leading to improved precision in BAO-based cosmological inference. The authors advocate adopting this framework in future large-scale structure analyses to better exploit data from DESI, Euclid, and similar surveys.
\n\n
在估计galaxy bias参数的微扰分析中，必须更谨慎地考虑cosmic variance。所提出的框架提供了一种原则性方法，将有限体积效应与内在bias分离，从而提高基于BAO的宇宙学推断精度。作者建议在未来的大尺度结构分析中采用该框架，以更充分地利用DESI、Euclid等巡天的数据。

Abstract: Current and forthcoming galaxy redshift surveys, such as DESI and Euclid, are going to bring cosmological analysis to an unprecedentedly exquisite level of precision in the determination of the cosmological parameters. However, these efforts require a high degree of control over theory and systematics, to obtain unbiased results. In this sense, the cosmic variance associated to finite-volume effects represents a major challenge and should adequately accounted for. In this work, we revisit the definition of cosmic variance and develop a novel framework to describe it using a `galaxy biasing' formalism. In particular, we use halo/galaxy Eulerian perturbation theory to relate the density field from an arbitrary cosmic realization to its counterpart having statistical properties reproducing the ensemble average, introducing a new set of bias parameters. We then apply this idea to the description of the non-linear shift of BAO, disentangling the source of uncertainty from cosmic variance and galaxy biasing associated with the measurement of the BAO scale. We finally check our analytical argument against cosmological variance-suppressed $N$-body simulations, finding an expected reduction in the uncertainty on the BAO peak position. We conclude that extra care should be used when inferring cosmological information from perturbative approaches involving the estimation of bias parameters and propose new practical strategies to optimally leverage the novel formulation of cosmic variance presented herein in cosmological analysis.

</details>

Result: The model demonstrates that weak dissipation ($\Gamma \\ll H$) in a standard quintessence framework can produce an effective equation of state that evolves across $w = -1$ into the phantom regime, matching the evolving dark energy signal reported by DESI, while avoiding ghost or gradient instabilities inherent in true phantom models.

该模型表明，在标准quintessence框架中引入微弱耗散（$\Gamma \\ll H$）即可产生一个有效状态方程，使其从$w = -1$演化进入phantom区域，与DESI报告的演化暗能量信号一致，同时避免了真实phantom模型中固有的ghost或梯度不稳定性。

Abstract: DESI results preferring an evolving dark energy component that appears to cross the phantom-divide in the recent past has raised a lot of interest in exploring the nature of dark energy.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: The analysis reveals that the cosmological upper bound on $\\sum m_\\nu$ is extremely sensitive to the functional form of the interaction $Q$. Abstract: Recent DESI observations indicate a deviation from the $Λ$CDM model, showing a preference for dynamical dark energy and thereby relaxing the upper limit on the neutrino mass within this framework. These results demonstrate that the upper bounds on $\sum m_ν$ exhibit profound sensitivity to the specific phenomenological formulation of the interaction term. While the I$Λ$CDM2 model ($Q \propto H ρ_{\mathrm{c}}$) substantially relaxes the stringent upper limit ($\sum m_ν < 0.129$ eV at 95% confidence level), notably the I$Λ$CDM3 model ($Q \propto H_0 ρ_{\mathrm{de}}$), severely compresses the allowed parameter space, yielding a highly restrictive bound of $\sum m_ν < 0.051$ eV. Furthermore, rigorous goodness-of-fit evaluations utilizing the Deviance Information Criterion and $Δχ^2_{\mathrm{MAP}}$ indicate that the current observational data statistically favor these mass-suppressing IDE models. This establishes an exacerbated statistical tension between the observationally preferred IDE scenarios and the normal hierarchy lower bound ($\sim 0.06$ eV) determined by terrestrial neutrino oscillation experiments.

标准宇宙学模型预测，在He II再电离于$z \\sim 3$左右结束后，星系际介质（IGM）应显著冷却，至$z = 0$时温度约为$T_0 \\sim 4000\\,\\text{K}$，多方指数$\\gamma \\sim 1.6$。然而，近期对宽Lyman-$\\alpha$吸收线的观测表明在低红移（$z < 0.5$）处温度远高于预期，挑战了这一标准图像。此外，推断出的氢光电离率与紫外背景（UV-background）合成模型之间存在差异，促使人们重新审视IGM的热力学与电离物理，包括未解析的物理过程（如湍流）的潜在贡献。

Method: The authors employ a machine-learning-based inference framework that leverages the joint Doppler parameter–column density ($b$–$N_{\\rm HI}$) distribution extracted from Lyman-$\\alpha$ forest decomposition of 82 archival HST/COS quasar spectra across four redshift bins ($0.06 < z < 0.48$). They infer the IGM thermal parameters ($T_0$, $\\gamma$) and the hydrogen photoionization rate ($\\Gamma_{\\rm HI}$) by comparing observed line widths to synthetic spectra generated from hydrodynamical simulations or analytic models. To test alternative explanations, they explore a grid of turbulent velocities ($v_{\\rm tur}$) and $\\Gamma_{\\rm HI}$ values, assessing whether unresolved turbulence can reproduce the observed $b$-parameters without invoking anomalously high temperatures. Instrumental effects are ruled out using higher-resolution STIS data.
\n\n
作者采用一种基于机器学习的推断框架，利用从82条存档的HST/COS类星体光谱中分解出的Lyman-$\\alpha$森林的多普勒参数–柱密度（$b$–$N_{\\rm HI}$）联合分布，在四个红移区间（$0.06 < z < 0.48$）内推断IGM的热力学参数（$T_0$、$\\gamma$）和氢光电离率（$\\Gamma_{\\rm HI}$）。他们通过将观测到的谱线宽度与流体动力学模拟或解析模型生成的合成光谱进行比较来实现推断。为检验替代理论，他们构建了一个湍流速度（$v_{\\rm tur}$）与$\\Gamma_{\\rm HI}$的参数网格，评估未解析的湍流是否能在不引入异常高温的情况下复现观测到的$b$参数。通过更高分辨率的STIS数据排除了仪器效应。

Result: At $z = 0.1$, the IGM temperature is measured as $\\log(T_0/\\text{K}) = 4.45^{+0.08}_{-0.12}$ ($T_0 \\approx 28,183^{+5700}_{-6804}\\,\\text{K}$) with $\\gamma = 1.06^{+0.13}_{-0.09}$, which is $\\sim 7\\sigma$ hotter than the canonical prediction of $\\log T_0 \\approx 3.60$ ($T_0 \\sim 4000\\,\\text{K}$). The inferred hydrogen photoionization rate is $\\log(\\Gamma_{\\rm HI}/\\text{s}^{-1}) = -13.70^{+0.10}_{-0.08}$, about $4\\sigma$ below UV-background model predictions ($\\sim -13.3$). A model incorporating standard thermal conditions plus unresolved turbulence with $v_{\\rm tur} \\simeq 15\\,\\text{km/s}$ successfully reproduces the observed line widths. STIS data confirm that instrumental broadening is insufficient to explain the results.
\n\n
在$z = 0.1$处，测得IGM温度为$\\log(T_0/\\text{K}) = 4.45^{+0.08}_{-0.12}$（$T_0 \\approx 28,183^{+5700}_{-6804}\\,\\text{K}$），多方指数$\\gamma = 1.06^{+0.13}_{-0.09}$，比标准预测值$\\log T_0 \\approx 3.60$（即$T_0 \\sim 4000\\,\\text{K}$）高出约$7\\sigma$。推断出的氢光电离率为$\\log(\\Gamma_{\\rm HI}/\\text{s}^{-1}) = -13.70^{+0.10}_{-0.08}$，比紫外背景模型预测值（约$-13.3$）低约$4\\sigma$。一个包含标准热状态并叠加未解析湍流（$v_{\\rm tur} \\simeq 15\\,\\text{km/s}$）的模型能成功复现观测到的谱线宽度。STIS数据证实仪器展宽不足以解释该结果。

Conclusion: The unexpectedly high temperatures and low photoionization rates in the low-$z$ IGM cannot be explained by standard post-reionization cooling models. While the data appear to require a hot, nearly isothermal IGM, an equally viable explanation is the presence of unresolved small-scale turbulence that artificially broadens absorption lines. This implies that either new, late-time heating mechanisms (e.g., from AGN feedback, dark matter decay, or cosmic rays) are operating, or that turbulence must be accounted for in IGM thermal inference. Future high-resolution spectroscopy and improved modeling of non-thermal broadening are essential to disentangle these possibilities.
\n\n
低红移IGM中意外的高温和低光电离率无法用标准的再电离后冷却模型解释。尽管数据似乎要求一个高温且近乎等温的IGM，但同样合理的解释是存在未解析的小尺度湍流，导致吸收线人为展宽。这意味着要么存在新的晚期加热机制（如AGN反馈、暗物质衰变或宇宙射线），要么必须在IGM热力学推断中考虑湍流效应。未来高分辨率光谱观测和对非热展宽的改进建模对于区分这两种可能性至关重要。

Abstract: We apply a machine-learning-based inference method that exploits the joint Doppler parameter-column density (b-NHI) distribution from Lya forest decomposition to measure the thermal and ionization state of the intergalactic medium (IGM) in four redshift bins spanning z = 0.06 to 0.48, using 82 archival quasar spectra from the Cosmic Origin Spectrograph (COS) on board Hubble Space Telescope (HST). Our results show that the low-z IGM (z < 0.5) is extremely hot and nearly isothermal, with log(T0/K) = 4.45 (+0.08 / -0.12) [T0 = 28183 (+5700 / -6804) K] and gamma = 1.06 (+0.13 / -0.09) at z = 0.1. This temperature lies approx 7sigma (and 7 times) above the canonical prediction (log T0 approx 3.60, i.e. T0 ~ 4000 K, with gamma ~ 1.6 at z = 0), where the IGM is expected to have cooled long after He II reionization. To investigate the discrepancy between these high temperatures and theoretical models, we assess the impact of small-scale turbulence. By exploring a parameter grid in turbulent velocity (vtur) and GammaHI, we find that a standard IGM thermal and ionization state combined with unresolved turbulence of vtur simeq 15 km s^-1 can successfully reproduce the observed line widths at z = 0.1. Comparisons with high-resolution Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS) expanded data indicate that the observed line widths are unlikely to be caused by instrumental resolution effects. Our findings suggest that either new heating mechanisms or unresolved turbulence are required to explain the unexpectedly broad Lya lines observed in the low-z IGM.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peculiar velocity reconstruction from galaxy positions is a key probe of gravitational dynamics and dark sector physics, but linear theory fails to capture nonlinear structure formation effects. Existing methods lack accuracy on small scales, limiting their utility for kSZ and other velocity-sensitive cosmological analyses. Machine learning offers a data-driven route to model these nonlinearities.

从星系位置重构本动速度是探测引力动力学和暗区物理的关键手段，但线性理论无法捕捉结构形成的非线性效应。现有方法在小尺度上精度不足，限制了其在kSZ及其他对速度敏感的宇宙学分析中的应用。机器学习提供了一种数据驱动的途径来建模这些非线性效应。

Method: The authors train two machine learning models—a Gradient Boosting Decision Tree (GBDT) and a Transformer—on multi-scale features extracted from mock galaxy catalogs to predict the residual $\\vec{v}_{\\text{true}} - \\vec{v}_{\\text{linear}}$ for both line-of-sight and transverse velocity components. The training data come from \\textsc{AbacusSummit} N-body simulations tailored to DESI LRG and ELG samples, evaluated in both periodic box and lightcone geometries. Redshift uncertainties mimicking Rubin LSST photometric errors are also incorporated.

Result: Both ML models outperform linear theory, with the Transformer achieving superior performance. Conclusion: This work establishes a robust machine learning framework for nonlinear peculiar velocity reconstruction from galaxy surveys, significantly enhancing the fidelity of velocity field inference. It paves the way for high-precision kSZ and related cosmological applications with upcoming surveys like DESI, Rubin LSST, Euclid, and Roman.

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of peculiar velocities from galaxy positions is important for probing the motion and evolution of large scale structure. They are sensitive to the cosmological effects of gravity and dark sector matter, and complement other velocity inference methods such as the kinematic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ) imprinted in the CMB. We show that machine learning methods improve velocity reconstruction by capturing nonlinear contributions. Specifically, we train both a gradient boosting decision tree (GBDT) and a Transformer using multi-scale features to predict the residual between the actual velocity and the estimate from linear theory for both the line-of-sight and transverse components. We evaluate our approach in both periodic box and, more realistic, lightcone settings using mock galaxy catalogs from the \textsc{AbacusSummit} simulations tailored to DESI spectroscopic surveys of luminous red galaxies (LRGs) and emission line galaxies (ELGs). We also assess the impact of redshift uncertainties such as those in Rubin LSST photometry. Both models significantly outperform the linear theory with the Transformer achieving the best performance. They more accurately recover the velocity power spectrum and maintain a higher cross-correlation with the true velocities across a wider range of spatial scales. Finally, we demonstrate two applications relevant to kSZ analyses: estimating cluster pairwise velocity correlations and stacked cluster density profiles. This machine learning framework for nonlinear velocity reconstruction opens up powerful new applications of survey data from DESI, Rubin LSST, Euclid and the Roman Space Telescope.

本文研究了含有自相互作用费米子暗物质（DM）的中子星所发出的连续引力波（CW）信号，该暗物质在星体内部诱导出非轴对称形变（“暗山”）。作者构建了一个理论框架，将暗物质积累与恒星椭率和转动惯量的增加联系起来，从而增强CW辐射。利用LIGO O3全天空搜索结果，他们首次对暗物质诱导的椭率施加了观测限制，并排除了暗物质质量–自耦合参数空间中的若干区域。未来探测器如Einstein Telescope和Cosmic Explorer预计将显著提升这些限制。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the possibility that isolated, rapidly rotating neutron stars could harbor accumulations of self-interacting fermionic dark matter, leading to anisotropic internal stress distributions that generate permanent quadrupolar deformations—so-called "dark mountains." Since CW searches are sensitive to such persistent deformations, they offer a unique probe of particle DM properties that is complementary to direct detection and collider experiments.

动机源于孤立的快速旋转中子星可能积聚自相互作用费米子暗物质，导致内部应力分布各向异性，从而产生永久性四极形变——即所谓的“暗山”。这些形变增强了恒星的椭率 $\\varepsilon$ 和转动惯量，进而提高所发射连续引力波（CW）的振幅。由于CW搜索对这类持久形变敏感，因此为探测粒子暗物质性质提供了独特手段，可补充直接探测和对撞机实验。

Method: The authors construct a formalism describing how self-interacting fermionic DM accumulates inside neutron stars and generates anisotropic pressure profiles, leading to equatorial deformations ("dark mountains"). They compute the resulting increase in the star’s moment of inertia and relate it to the CW strain amplitude $h_0 \\propto \\varepsilon I_{zz} f^2 / d$, where $\\varepsilon$ is the ellipticity, $I_{zz}$ the moment of inertia, $f$ the GW frequency, and $d$ the distance. Using upper limits on $h_0$ from LIGO O3 all-sky CW searches, they invert this relation to constrain $\\varepsilon$ as a function of GW frequency. They then map these constraints onto the DM parameter space—specifically the DM mass $m_\\chi \\in [0.1, 10]$ GeV and self-interaction coupling $g$—by modeling how $\\varepsilon$ depends on $g$ and $m_\\chi$. Projections for next-generation detectors (Einstein Telescope, Cosmic Explorer) are also computed using expected sensitivity improvements.

作者构建了一个形式体系，描述自相互作用费米子暗物质如何在中子星内部积聚并产生各向异性压强分布，从而导致赤道形变（“暗山”）。他们计算了由此引起的转动惯量增加，并将其与CW应变振幅关联：$h_0 \\propto \\varepsilon I_{zz} f^2 / d$，其中 $\\varepsilon$ 为椭率，$I_{zz}$ 为转动惯量，$f$ 为引力波频率，$d$ 为距离。利用LIGO O3全天空CW搜索对 $h_0$ 的上限，他们反推得到作为引力波频率函数的 $\\varepsilon$ 限制。随后，通过建模 $\\varepsilon$ 对暗物质质量 $m_\\chi \\in [0.1, 10]$ GeV 和自相互作用耦合常数 $g$ 的依赖关系，将这些限制映射到暗物质参数空间。此外，还基于预期灵敏度提升，计算了下一代探测器（Einstein Telescope、Cosmic Explorer）的投影限制。

They exclude DM self-coupling strengths $g \\gtrsim 10^{-5.5}$ for $\\varepsilon = 10^{-7}$ at $d = 1$ kpc, and $g \\gtrsim 10^{-4}$ for $\\varepsilon = 10^{-9}$ at $d = 10$ kpc, across $m_\\chi \\in [0.1, 10]$ GeV. For next-generation detectors, they project exclusions reaching $g \\gtrsim 10^{-6}$ for $\\varepsilon = 10^{-7}$ at $d = 10$ kpc, significantly expanding the accessible parameter space. Conclusion: Continuous gravitational-wave searches provide a powerful and direct observational window into the existence of self-interacting fermionic dark matter inside neutron stars through its gravitational imprint—namely, the generation of "dark mountains" that enhance CW emission. 连续引力波搜索通过其引力印记——即产生增强CW辐射的“暗山”——为探测中子星内部自相互作用费米子暗物质的存在提供了强大而直接的观测窗口。当前LIGO数据已限制了此前未被探索的暗物质参数空间区域，而未来探测器将大幅提升灵敏度，使通过引力波天文学对粒子暗物质模型进行严格检验成为可能。

Abstract: We investigate continuous gravitational-wave (CW) emission from rapidly rotating, non-axisymmetric, isolated neutron stars admixed with self-interacting fermionic dark matter (DM) and hosting DM-induced equatorial deformations (``dark mountains''). In particular, we develop a formalism that describes how DM accumulation inside the star changes its structure, how dark mountains arise from an anisotropic distribution of DM inside it, and how the star's moment of inertia and thus the amplitude of its GW emission is increased compared to that of an ordinary neutron star. Method: The authors model the gravitational capture of cosmic background radiation (neutrinos and photons) by PBHs as an effective energy transfer process that modifies the standard continuity equations for radiation and PBH densities. They incorporate this modified energy budget into the Friedmann equation to compute the altered expansion history of the universe. The resulting perturbations in the CMB temperature and E-mode polarization power spectra are then compared with Planck data and projected future CMB measurements (up to $\ell_{\rm max}=7000$) to derive constraints on the PBH abundance parameter $f_{\rm pbh}$ across a wide mass range ($10^{13}–10^{18} M_\odot$).

作者将原初黑洞对宇宙背景辐射（中微子和光子）的引力捕获建模为一种有效能量转移过程，从而修正辐射与PBH密度的标准连续性方程。他们将这一修正后的能量预算纳入Friedmann方程，以计算被改变的宇宙膨胀历史。随后，他们计算由此产生的CMB温度与E模偏振功率谱扰动，并与Planck数据及未来CMB测量（最高至$\ell_{\rm max}=7000$）进行对比，从而在宽质量范围（$10^{13}–10^{18} M_\odot$）内对PBH丰度参数$f_{\rm pbh}$施加限制。

Result: Current Planck CMB data constrain the PBH abundance to $f_{\rm pbh}\lesssim 10^{-1}$ for masses above $10^{15} M_\odot$, with tighter bounds expected for higher masses. Conclusion: Gravitational capture of cosmic background radiation by extraordinarily massive primordial black holes leaves imprints on the CMB anisotropies. These imprints provide a theoretically clean and observationally complementary probe of PBH abundance at the high-mass end, with significant potential for improvement from next-generation CMB experiments.
\n\n
极高质量原初黑洞对宇宙背景辐射的引力捕获会在CMB各向异性中留下印记。这种效应为高质量端PBH丰度提供了一种理论清晰且观测上互补的探针，并有望通过下一代CMB实验获得显著改进。

Abstract: We explore the capture of neutrinos and photons in the cosmic neutrino and photon background by primordial black holes (PBHs). We model this phenomenon as a gravitational interaction that effectively modifies the continuity equations for radiation and PBH densities and the cosmic expansion history. We find that the observability of this modified cosmic history is highly sensitive to PBH mass, and only extraordinarily massive PBHs would leave observable trace on the temperature and E-mode polarization of the cosmic microwave background (CMB). Specifically, Planck data restrict PBH abundance to $f_{\rm pbh}\lesssim 10^{-1}$ for PBH masses above $10^{15} M_\odot$, getting considerably tighter for higher masses. We expect substantial improvement as high-resolution measurements of larger CMB multipoles become available. A future cosmic-variance-limited experiment, with $\ell_{\rm max}=7000$, would set $f_{\rm pbh}\lesssim 10^{-1}-8\times 10^{-5}$ (for the fiducial $Λ$CDM cosmology) across $10^{13}-10^{18}M_\odot$. These constraints would be comparable to the current limits at the high-mass end of the spectrum [Carr et al, 2026]. The gravitational interaction of PBHS with the cosmic background radiation and its imprints on CMB would thus provide an independent complementary probe of extraordinarily massive PBH abundance.

</details>


### [60] [Compressed \emph{Gaussian} likelihood for the \textit{Planck} low-$\ell$ data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05166)
*Nanoom Lee*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The authors construct a compressed Gaussian likelihood for Planck CMB low-$\ell$ E-mode polarization data based on the Sroll2 likelihood, enabling its use in Fisher matrix analyses. By leveraging the offset log-normal form of the original likelihood and validating against full MCMC with Planck and ACT DR6 data, they show excellent agreement in parameter constraints and uncertainties. They release a lightweight Python package, planck-gaussian-lowl, for easy integration into Gaussian-likelihood-based cosmological analyses.
\n\n
作者基于Sroll2似然构建了一个压缩的高斯似然，用于Planck CMB低多极（low-$\ell$）E模偏振数据，使其可用于Fisher矩阵分析。通过利用原始似然的offset log-normal形式，并结合Planck与ACT DR6数据进行完整MCMC验证，他们在参数约束和不确定性方面展现出优异的一致性。他们发布了一个轻量级Python包planck-gaussian-lowl，便于将Planck CMB低多极数据集成到基于高斯似然的宇宙学分析中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The non-Gaussian nature of the Planck CMB low-$\ell$ TT and EE likelihoods prevents their direct use in Fisher matrix methods, which assume an analytic Gaussian $χ^2$. This limitation hinders applications such as Fisher-bias formalism and forecast analyses that rely on Gaussian approximations. The paper aims to overcome this by constructing a compressed Gaussian proxy likelihood compatible with such frameworks.
\n\n
Planck CMB低多极TT和EE似然的非高斯特性阻碍了其在Fisher矩阵方法中的直接应用，因为这些方法假设存在解析形式的高斯$χ^2$。这一限制影响了诸如Fisher-bias formalism和基于高斯近似的预测分析等应用。本文旨在通过构建一个与这些框架兼容的压缩高斯代理似然来克服该问题。

Method: The authors exploit the fact that the $χ^2$ of an offset log-normal likelihood becomes Gaussian in the log-transformed power spectrum amplitudes. They compress the Sroll2 likelihood into a small number of piecewise offset log-normal functions without performing an explicit change of variables. The compressed likelihood is validated through MCMC analyses combined with Planck and ACT DR6 data, and its Fisher matrix uncertainty estimates are compared against full MCMC posteriors.
\n\n
作者利用offset log-normal似然的$χ^2$在对数变换后的功率谱振幅空间中呈高斯形式这一性质。他们将Sroll2似然压缩为少量分段的offset log-normal函数，且无需显式变量变换。通过结合Planck与ACT DR6数据的MCMC分析对该压缩似然进行验证，并将其Fisher矩阵不确定性估计与完整MCMC后验分布进行比较。

Result: The compressed Gaussian likelihood shows excellent agreement with the full Sroll2 likelihood across all ΛCDM parameters and in extended cosmological models. Fisher matrix uncertainty estimates derived from the compressed likelihood closely match the full MCMC posteriors. The resulting lightweight Python package, planck-gaussian-lowl, enables straightforward inclusion of Planck low-$\ell$ CMB data in Gaussian-likelihood-based analyses.
\n\n
该压缩高斯似然在所有ΛCDM参数及扩展宇宙学模型中均与完整Sroll2似然高度一致。由该压缩似然导出的Fisher矩阵不确定性估计与完整MCMC后验分布非常吻合。最终得到的轻量级Python包planck-gaussian-lowl可便捷地将Planck低多极CMB数据纳入基于高斯似然的分析中。

Conclusion: The paper successfully constructs and validates a compressed Gaussian likelihood for Planck low-$\ell$ E-mode data, overcoming the incompatibility of the original non-Gaussian likelihood with Fisher matrix methods. This enables efficient and accurate cosmological parameter estimation and forecasting within Gaussian frameworks, with the public release of planck-gaussian-lowl facilitating broader adoption in the community.
\n\n
本文成功构建并验证了用于Planck低多极E模数据的压缩高斯似然，解决了原始非高斯似然与Fisher矩阵方法不兼容的问题。这使得在高斯框架下进行高效且精确的宇宙学参数估计与预测成为可能，而planck-gaussian-lowl的公开发布将促进该方法在学界的广泛应用。

Abstract: We present a compressed \emph{Gaussian} likelihood for the \textit{Planck} CMB low-$\ell$ E-mode polarization data, constructed from the \texttt{Sroll2} likelihood which provides the tightest constraint on the reionization optical depth $τ$ to date. The non-Gaussian form of CMB low-$\ell$ TT and EE likelihoods makes them incompatible with Fisher matrix analyses that require an analytic Gaussian $χ^2$, such as the Fisher-bias formalism and Fisher forecasts. We show that the $χ^2$ of an offset log-normal likelihood takes a Gaussian form in the log-transformed power spectrum amplitudes, and can therefore serve as a proxy for the true Gaussian likelihood of this variable in Fisher matrix analyses, without any explicit change of variables. Building on this, we compress the \texttt{Sroll2} likelihood into a small number of piecewise offset log-normal functions and validate it against the full \texttt{Sroll2} likelihood via MCMC combined with \textit{Planck} and ACT DR6 data, finding excellent agreement across all $Λ$CDM parameters and in extended cosmological models. We further demonstrate that Fisher matrix uncertainty estimates from our compressed likelihood agree well with the full MCMC posteriors. We release our compressed likelihood \texttt{planck-gaussian-lowl}, a lightweight Python package incorporating the compressed low-$\ell$ TT likelihood from previous work, allowing a straightforward incorporation of the Planck CMB low-$\ell$ data into any Gaussian-likelihood-based analysis. The package is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/nanoomlee/planck-gaussian-lowl}{github.com/nanoomlee/planck-gaussian-lowl}.

</details>
